# The Operative Centre Method
## Canonical growing guide for a new GPT — Edition 1, A-001–A-032

**Compiled:** 21 October 2026, Europe/Dublin.  
**Scope:** the working method, its vocabulary, decision constraints, and the complete first 32 persisted investigations, including A-007 revision 02.  
**Purpose:** let a new GPT understand and continue the work without having to reconstruct the conversation.

This is a consolidated teaching and operating guide. It preserves the controlling definitions and protocol, explains their use, and cross-references the decisions actually recorded. Its explanations do not amend canonical definitions. Its examples are research findings, not an answer key proving that every sentence has one recoverable cell.

**Important correction to a shorthand description of progress:** 32 first-pass investigations are complete; that does not mean 32 uniquely established classifications. A-002 remains contested between 05 and 23; A-005 remains contested between 24 and 42. A-007's current interpretation is revision 02, Cell 40; its original 19/20/21 dispute is historical. Other selected cells retain the status and limitations in their records. A-013 was accepted in collaborative review, which is distinct from independent replication.

# 1. The method stated

> **Let the sentence determine its Operative Centre; confirm and freeze that Centre; let the frozen Centre determine the dimensional conjunction; test rivals; preserve the result, including unresolved distinctions. Never let a desired cell determine the Centre retrospectively.**

### Gate One: establish the Centre before taxonomy

1. Retrieve the exact sentence, stable corpus ID and source version. Verify that it is complete enough to investigate. Preserve its modality, scope, qualifications, negation and dependencies. No completion, no coordinate.
2. Trace its unfolding in ordinary language: what each contribution supplies, what remains to be specified, and what completes its point. Distinguish the sentence's achievement from events described in the world.
3. Propose at least three serious Centre formulations. Seek the **smallest complete governing determination**. Reject accounts that omit governing contributions, merely name a topic, or add unsupported explanation.
4. Apply deletion, alteration, narrowing, reversal, dependency and sufficiency-location tests. State what each test establishes and what it does not establish.
5. Answer the six confirmation questions in pre-taxonomic language:
   - What was unresolved?
   - What changed?
   - What made it sufficient?
   - Where is sufficiency located?
   - What remains achieved?
   - What would invalidate it?
6. Complete the four holding tests: exact identity; constitutive dependency; operative continuation; minimal invalidation.
7. Write and compare both achieved-identity formulations:
   - **What has been established is ___.**
   - **What obtains through what is ___.**
   Determine whether either omits part of the completed point or adds a dependence. Necessity of a term alone does not settle this.
8. Write the positive holding statement:
   > What remains achieved is ___; it holds as this achievement because ___; removing ___ would change/defeat it in the following way: ___.
9. Check coverage, minimality, non-invention and determinacy. Pursue material ambiguities before freezing; if ambiguity survives, specify it honestly.
10. Freeze the complete Centre Confirmation Record, not just a one-line paraphrase. Declare:
   > **OPERATIVE CENTRE CONFIRMED — SOURCE SENTENCE CLOSED.**

### The One-Way Threshold

After that declaration, the original sentence is no longer evidence for deciding the cell. Do not reread it, quote a useful phrase from it, silently paraphrase it differently, or amend the Centre to settle a classification dispute. Use the frozen record alone as sentence-specific input.

The original remains available for provenance and later publication. That does not license reopening it inside Gate Two.

### Gate Two: derive and test the conjunction

11. Use the complete frozen record and the canonical control. Examine all six Transformation Patterns, all three Completion Topologies and all three Persistence Modes.
12. Derive **one unified completion-event** constrained simultaneously by all three dimensions. A table may explain the constraints one at a time; that presentation order is not a sequence of semantic operations.
13. Exclude alternatives through specific contradictions with the frozen record. Derive the coordinate before using the cell's canonical name as a lookup.
14. Test the strongest surviving rival, normally including a one-dimension neighbour and any material cross-family rival. Explain the exact difference in achievement, not just a different label.
15. Report the cell number, exact canonical name, full conjunction, unified account, rival, discriminant, status, confidence and remaining diligence. If multiple cells remain supported, record the surviving alternatives and the unresolved structural distinction. Do not manufacture uniqueness.

### Preserve and communicate

16. Save the full diligence record before indexing it. Verify the saved content, append its register entry, and verify the index.
17. Where continuing the repository workflow, publish the exact source, current result/status and explanatory receipt; link the full diligence, update public indexes, and verify links. Distinguish successful repository writes from verified public deployment.
18. Make substantive corrections as new dated revisions with a new Gate One. Keep earlier frozen records intact. Update current indexes without erasing history.

# 2. Vocabulary and object of investigation

The exact canonical definitions appear in [Section 10](#10-canonical-definitions-conjunction-and-lookup). The explanations here support their use; they are not substitute definitions.

| Term | Working meaning and constraint |
|---|---|
| Intelligibility | The achievement of a point that can be understood as one completed whole, rather than an accumulation of associated words. |
| Unitus | A completed sentence considered as one unit of intelligibility. It is not a classification of its subject matter. |
| Unfolding | How the sentence's contributions develop and become sufficient together. Word order matters, but the method is not simply grammatical parsing. |
| Landing | Where the sentence's developed point becomes intelligibly complete. The final word is not automatically the governing contribution. |
| Operative Centre / Centre | The minimal governing determination through which the unfolding becomes sufficient and holds as achieved. It states what the whole achieves. |
| Smallest | No unnecessary content is included. It does not mean shortest possible wording. |
| Complete | No essential contribution, qualification or dependency is omitted. |
| Governing | The account explains why the necessary contributions belong to the same completed point. |
| Opening constraint / unresolvedness | What remains undetermined in the unfolding before completion. Do not invent a historical dispute, ignorance or defective worldly state simply to supply one. |
| Constitutive organisation | How the contributions make completion sufficient. This is distinct from the identity of what remains achieved. |
| Location of sufficiency | Where or across what completion becomes sufficient. It is not a count of grammatical subjects or nouns. |
| Mode of holding | What the completed achievement consists in and why it remains this achievement. This pre-taxonomic account must precede selection of a Persistence Mode. |
| Constitutive dependency | A dependence belonging to the identity of the result, rather than merely to the evidence, wording or prerequisites used to establish it. |
| Operative continuation | A capacity, function or consequential difference actually established as the completed point. Potential can be established without its exercise being asserted. |
| Counterfactual / invalidation | A controlled variation exposing what is indispensable. Losing a term can defeat many forms; it does not by itself identify a dimension. |
| Frozen Centre Confirmation Record | The full Gate One transfer: exact Centre, opening constraint, completion, organisation, sufficiency location, holding statement, decisive variation and confirmation checks. |
| Dimensional Conjunction | Simultaneous reciprocal constraints on one completion-event. Not three properties added together, and not a fourth dimension. |
| Coordinate | Transformation Pattern × Completion Topology × Persistence Mode. |
| Cell / crystallization | A numbered coordinate and its canonical name. The name is a compact crystallization, not an extra primitive definition or a literal script for the source. |
| Diligence | The preserved tests, reasons, rivals, provenance, limitations and future questions that make a resolution inspectable. |
| Receipt | The readable public account of a sentence's current resolution, linked to its full diligence. |
| Candidate / contested / revised / abstained | Different research statuses. A chosen candidate is not independently proved; a contested completed assay still counts as worked. |

### Keep three events distinct

1. An event or condition in the world.
2. The sentence's linguistic development and completion.
3. A reader's resulting understanding.

A sentence about motion is not automatically Efficacy; a reader learning anything is not automatically Opening; an informative assertion is not automatically Settlement. The target is the governing achievement the particular sentence makes intelligible.

For example, A-012 describes migration but its frozen Centre establishes temporal precedence. A-027 describes a perceptual effect but its frozen identity is magnitude through a frame. A-013 establishes a potential difference to later noticing. The topic alone cannot distinguish these cases.

# 3. The method explained

## 3.1 Why a Centre precedes a coordinate

An early cell guess changes what a GPT notices. It can then write a plausible paraphrase containing exactly the features needed for its preferred cell. The Centre gate interrupts that circularity.

A Centre must account for the whole sentence before classification. “A desert,” “ancient nature,” “a relation,” and “a useful question” do not yet identify completed achievements. In A-011 the correction and its particular basis both mattered. In A-012 an unspecified claim of antiquity was weaker than precedence over every human map. In A-013 replacing “can change” with “changes” would falsely turn potential into occurrence.

A user suggestion is a hypothesis, not evidence of its own correctness. A-013's proposed 19 was tested and the investigation selected 21; A-003's collaborative influence remains disclosed. Do not claim independence merely because you disagree with a suggestion.

## 3.2 How to follow unfolding

Read each contribution for its role in the completed point. Identify setting, bearer, predicate, scope, contrast, support and continuation without assuming that any grammatical category governs.

A setting may merely locate an achievement, as the tundra does in A-012. A shared place may instead organise an achieved gathering, as recorded in A-015 and A-021. This must be argued from sufficiency, not inferred from a place name.

Do not make “what was unresolved?” into a fictional prior event. The source can leave something unspecified until its continuation supplies it without asserting that anyone previously misunderstood it. A-004 does not establish an earlier mistaken belief; A-029 does not establish an obligation that was subsequently discharged.

## 3.3 How competing Centres discipline the reading

Keep alternatives materially different: bare topic or activity; complete candidate; tempting over-interpretation. Explain each omission or addition.

A-007 is the central correction. Its first pass privileged “carries” and narrowed the investigation to a carrier/content relation. Comparison with A-003 exposed inconsistent handling of an articulated whole. Revision 02 investigated the source anew and confirmed the seed's unified instructional endowment, selecting 40.

The lesson is to test the governing achievement beyond the grammatical verb. It is not “all lists are Integration,” “all seeds are Cell 40,” or “replace a disputed Centre until its cell agrees with another case.” The old record remains intact because the correction changed the Centre, not merely the final label.

## 3.4 What controlled variations prove

Delete a contribution to test coverage. Alter it to distinguish its particular role. Narrow a quantifier to test scope. Reverse an ordering or influence to test direction. Separate support from evidence and a bearer from a completion locus.

A-012's “any” has universal comparative reach; one map as old as the routes defeats that ordering. A-013's “everyone” specifies the reach of potential influence, without alone making completion Distributed. A-030's “may” and “harder” must survive: possible increased difficulty is not inevitable loss or impossibility.

A deletion result often has limited force. Removing the map benchmark loses A-012's particular ordering; removing a comparison term can also lose a Bearing achievement. That shared vulnerability does not discriminate them. A positive holding account is needed.

A test variation is an analytic instrument, not new factual evidence about the world. Do not report an invented counterexample as an observed historical fact.

## 3.5 Why the holding tests are demanding

“What remains achieved?” asks about identity, not merely duration or usefulness. Three readings can be confused:

- A determination is established as a result.
- A result is what it is through a constitutive counterpart, support or frame.
- A result consists in an operative capacity, function or consequential continuation.

Use ordinary language to establish which account preserves the whole point. Only later apply canonical labels.

A-001, A-012 and A-031 favour established temporal configurations. Their references identify the exact ordering without automatically making persistence Bearing. This is not a general rule that temporal comparisons select Standing.

A-010 retains preservation through remembered unwritable knowledge. A-024 retains greatest significance under an obscuring condition. A-027 retains perceived magnitude through winter silence. Their current records positively identify the relevant support, condition or frame.

A-023 instead records a degree specified through a counterpart, while A-016 records contrasted significance within an encounter. Both remain useful audits of the same difficult boundary. Their comparative wording alone would not settle it.

**Bearing need not be physical causation, reciprocal influence, a sustaining mechanism, or an extra object.** Its definition includes contrast, condition, source and frame. Conversely, all assertions needing context or two terms do not thereby become Bearing.

## 3.6 Potential, actual performance and efficacy

An unexercised capacity can still be an established capacity. Do not exclude Efficacy solely because no action has yet occurred. Equally, “can” does not automatically establish operative capacity: A-031 records possible comparative endurance.

The current distinctions include:

| Contrast | What the frozen records preserve |
|---|---|
| A-013 versus A-031 | Potential redirection of continuation versus a possible temporal ordering. |
| A-007 revision 02 versus A-013 | Action-directed instructional content as a whole versus an explicitly attributed potential influence. |
| A-010 versus A-019 | Availability held through remembered support versus restoration of practical functioning and possibilities. |
| A-024 versus A-025 | Conditional significance versus an expressly enabling finding function. |
| A-027 versus A-028 | Magnitude constituted through a perceptual frame versus joint effects on travel. |

Do not turn these into keyword rules. The source-specific identity must decide. A-020 explicitly includes a helping function; its classification cannot be defended by pretending that function is absent. The recorded claim is that the contrast governs the whole.

## 3.7 Topology is the constitution of sufficiency

Focal does not mean singular, Distributed does not mean plural, and Release does not mean negative.

A-007 has a single physical bearer but the revised record places completion across instructional contributions. A-013 has everyone as recipients but completion gathers at an initiating source. A-028 has two effects but records one source of consequential influence. A-018's strongest topology rival asks whether the boundary or the span across counterparts governs completion.

A-003 and revised A-007 select Distributed wholes; A-015, A-017 and A-021 select Focal gatherings or expressions. This is a substantive boundary for further testing: co-location alone is not enough to establish Integration, and singular expression alone is not enough to establish Focal. Preserve the actual locus argument and its diligence.

For Release, name the former governor and explain its loss of determining force. A-011 records displacement of an inadequate description. A-022 establishes non-governance by calendars without a previous calendar authority being removed. A-025's omission is not automatically Release. A-008's reopening does not by itself establish loss of governance under its chosen reading.

## 3.8 Transformation families must identify the same achievement

Differentiation makes a difference determinate; Relating establishes a governing bearing or ordering; Integration concerns roles in a whole. These can overlap in verbal description. The question is which accounts for the full recorded completion.

A-002 deliberately preserves Differentiation versus Relating as contested. A-005 preserves Relating versus Integration as contested. These are examples of the method refusing an unsupported answer, not gaps to conceal.

Repair requires a criterion-relative failure, not the word “again” or restoration alone. A-019's practical recovery is compared with Opening. A-025 favours improved findability over a generic effect. Settlement requires a live demand discharged; ordinary assertional completion is not sufficient.

This sample does not cover all 54 cells or all six families equally. Do not infer that unselected forms are impossible, invalid or unnecessary.

## 3.9 Simultaneous derivation and rival tests

Although an explanation may use separate rows for the three dimensions, each must constrain the same achieved point. Finish with one ordinary-language sentence that expresses the unity. If three different achievements are needed to justify three values, the conjunction has failed.

Test neighbours by asking what would have to be different in the frozen achievement. For A-013, 19 versus 21 concerns established connection versus potential consequential influence. For A-012, 22 versus 23 concerns established ordering versus comparative constitution. For A-007 revision 02, 40 versus 41 versus 42 distinguishes articulated whole, mutual-support identity and joint operative capacity.

A name cannot add evidence: “Responsive return” need not mean a literal reply; “Relational cycle” cannot supply an unstated feedback loop; “Pivoted balance” cannot supply equilibrium; “Load transfer” cannot supply a literal shifted load; “Condition reversal” cannot supply a mechanical reversal.

If the frozen record cannot decide, record that fact. If the source interpretation needs changing, begin a new Gate One in a separate revision. A freeze is an audit boundary, not proof that the analysis is correct.

# 4. Constraints for a new GPT

Use this as an operating contract.

1. **Do not classify topics.** A river, seed, school, road or hospital has no automatic cell.
2. **Do not classify by syntax or keywords.** “Carries,” “because,” “can,” “while,” “again,” “not,” a singular subject or a list can cue investigation; none supplies a coordinate.
3. **Do not pre-load the answer into the Centre.** Keep canonical axis terms and guessed cells out of Gate One's reasoning.
4. **Do not confuse concise with incomplete.** Preserve universal scope, possibility, direction and governing support even when these lengthen the Centre.
5. **Do not invent a premise to obtain a family.** No prior error for Repair, restricted access for Opening, live demand for Settlement or displaced authority for Release unless supported.
6. **Do not equate term-necessity with Bearing or noun-plurality with Distributed.**
7. **Do not require physical causation or detailed mechanisms for Bearing.** Do not invent them either.
8. **Do not infer Efficacy from usefulness, motion or change alone.** Do not require execution when potential capacity is the established point.
9. **Do not discard inconvenient content.** A-020's helping, A-028's two effects and A-030's restricted modality remain essential even if a simplified rival would be easier to classify.
10. **Do not import domain knowledge as unspoken source content.** No pollination chain for A-026, genetic mechanism for A-007, navigation success for A-024, legal enforcement for A-031 or scarcity explanation for A-011.
11. **Do not let metaphor license literal machinery.** Bound the reading before freezing.
12. **Do not reopen the source to decide a post-freeze rival.**
13. **Do not use another sentence as an answer template.** Comparisons diagnose inconsistencies; the candidate must still survive its own tests.
14. **Do not suppress disagreement.** A-002 and A-005 must remain contested until a separately documented investigation justifies a change.
15. **Do not erase corrections.** A-007 revision 02 supersedes its interpretation while retaining the first pass.
16. **Do not call this archive blind.** Reading its examples exposes the outcomes. A later independent blind experiment needs fresh contexts and the appropriate canonical instrument prompts.
17. **Do not confuse collaborative acceptance with validation.** A-013's acceptance is part of provenance, not independent reliability evidence.
18. **Do not claim all other 53 cells have been empirically excluded merely because one is preferred.** State structural exclusions and the actual residual uncertainty.
19. **Do not count revisions as new sentences or contested assays as unworked.**
20. **Do not report saved, indexed, deployed or verified without evidence of the corresponding action.**

# 5. Cross-case decision map

Each link leads to the complete record embedded below. These are case-derived constraints, subordinate to the exact canonical definitions.

| Decision boundary | Cases | Constraint carried forward |
|---|---|---|
| Established temporal configuration / constitutive comparison | [A-001](#case-a-001), [A-012](#case-a-012), [A-023](#case-a-023), [A-031](#case-a-031) | Retain the exact benchmark; compare positive identities. Temporal and comparative wording do not settle persistence. |
| Differentiation / Relating | [A-002](#case-a-002), [A-016](#case-a-016), [A-020](#case-a-020), [A-032](#case-a-032) | Determine whether the whole achieves a contextual distinction or a governing relation. Preserve a surviving boundary. |
| Relating / Integration | [A-003](#case-a-003), [A-005](#case-a-005), [A-007 revision](#case-a-007-revision-02), [A-021](#case-a-021) | Distinguish connection, inventory and contributions to a whole. Do not add functional roles to force Integration. |
| Physical bearer / completion locus | [A-007 revision](#case-a-007-revision-02), [A-013](#case-a-013), [A-017](#case-a-017), [A-018](#case-a-018), [A-028](#case-a-028) | Ask why completion is sufficient there or across those terms; count neither subjects nor effects. |
| Composition / mutual support / joint capacity | [A-003](#case-a-003), [A-007 revision](#case-a-007-revision-02), [A-015](#case-a-015), [A-017](#case-a-017), [A-021](#case-a-021) | A completed whole does not automatically imply sustained interdependence or effective operation. |
| Supported availability / consequential operation | [A-006](#case-a-006), [A-009](#case-a-009), [A-010](#case-a-010), [A-019](#case-a-019) | Identify what remains achieved rather than classifying any cause as Efficacy. |
| Potential consequence / descriptive possibility | [A-004](#case-a-004), [A-013](#case-a-013), [A-023](#case-a-023), [A-030](#case-a-030), [A-031](#case-a-031) | Preserve modality; a possible ordering and a capacity to change continuation are different recorded achievements. |
| Conditional significance / enabling function / perceptual frame | [A-014](#case-a-014), [A-024](#case-a-024), [A-025](#case-a-025), [A-027](#case-a-027) | Do not upgrade evidence or significance into successful action; do not ignore explicit function. |
| Release / absence or negation | [A-004](#case-a-004), [A-005](#case-a-005), [A-008](#case-a-008), [A-011](#case-a-011), [A-022](#case-a-022), [A-025](#case-a-025), [A-029](#case-a-029) | Name former governance and its loss; absence, independence and omission alone do not establish it. |
| Repair / Opening / restored relation | [A-008](#case-a-008), [A-011](#case-a-011), [A-019](#case-a-019), [A-025](#case-a-025) | A recovery, correction and enlargement must be distinguished through the governing criterion. |
| Directed influence / distributed development / feedback | [A-006](#case-a-006), [A-009](#case-a-009), [A-013](#case-a-013), [A-026](#case-a-026), [A-028](#case-a-028) | Do not supply a loop from a cell name; preserve any constitutive span or history actually recorded. |
| Established independence / contextual significance | [A-022](#case-a-022), [A-029](#case-a-029), [A-032](#case-a-032) | Distinguish the completed contrast from identity-through-context; do not decide by deletion alone. |
| Metaphor and inference limits | [A-002](#case-a-002), [A-003](#case-a-003), [A-014](#case-a-014), [A-016](#case-a-016), [A-018](#case-a-018), [A-026](#case-a-026), [A-027](#case-a-027) | Interpret enough to state the completed point; do not add literal memory, exchange machinery, birth, equilibrium, pollination mechanics or physical amplification. |
| Honest non-uniqueness and revision | [A-002](#case-a-002), [A-005](#case-a-005), [A-007 first pass](#case-a-007), [A-007 revision](#case-a-007-revision-02), [A-013](#case-a-013) | Keep contested status, superseded history, collaborative influence and limits visible. |

# 6. Reusable investigation template

Copy this template for a new investigation. Fill Gate One before consulting the lookup for this case.

```markdown
# [ID] — [first pass / revision number]
Date / assessor:
Status:
Exact source / version / source identifier:
Prior record and revision trigger:
Exposure / collaborative input / blind or non-blind conditions:

## Gate One
Exact source:
Unfolding:
Candidate Centre 1 / test:
Candidate Centre 2 / test:
Candidate Centre 3 / test:

Deletion:
Alteration:
Narrowing:
Reversal:
Dependency:
Sufficiency-location:
Limits of these tests:

### Six questions
What was unresolved?
What changed?
What made it sufficient?
Where is sufficiency located?
What remains achieved?
What would invalidate it?

### Holding tests
Identity:
Constitutive dependency:
Operative continuation (if established):
Minimal invalidation:

### Achieved-identity discrimination
What has been established is:
What obtains through what is:
Which preserves the whole? What would the other omit or add?
Remaining ambiguity and further pre-freeze tests:

### Frozen Centre Confirmation Record
Provenance:
Confirmed Operative Centre:
Opening constraint:
Achieved completion:
Constitutive organisation:
Location of sufficiency:
Mode of holding:
What remains achieved is ___; it holds as this achievement because ___;
removing ___ would change/defeat it in the following way: ___.
Decisive counterfactual:
Coverage:
Minimality:
Non-invention:
Determinacy / exact surviving ambiguity:

OPERATIVE CENTRE CONFIRMED — SOURCE SENTENCE CLOSED.

## Gate Two — frozen record only
One unified completion:
All six Transformation values, with structural reasons:
All three Topology values, with structural reasons:
All three Persistence values, with structural reasons:
Simultaneous conjunction:
Canonical cell number and name:
Strongest one-dimension neighbour:
Material cross-family rival:
Exact discriminant / why a rival survives:
Status / confidence / uniqueness limits:
Diligence questions:
Revision links:

## Persistence and publication
Full record saved and content verified:
Append-only register updated and verified:
Public receipt and linked full diligence updated:
Public deployment / links actually verified:
Next queue target:
```

# 7. Growth and revision rules

Treat this edition as a dated snapshot and a reusable learning file. Live authorities can advance; do not assume that its latest ID remains current.

- Preserve the ordering: method stated; explanation; constraints; decision map; register; exact references; complete records.
- Add each new case to the register, relevant boundary rows and full archive. Add a rule only when the case exposes a real distinction. Mark it as a case-derived constraint unless the canonical authority itself changes.
- Keep exact source and frozen analysis separately identifiable. Do not silently “improve” archived wording.
- On revision, append the new full record, mark which interpretation is current, link its predecessor, explain the trigger and update public current views. Preserve the old reasoning.
- Record source file identifiers and versions. If the protocol or canonical control changes, issue a new edition and describe the change rather than silently replacing the older basis.
- Maintain the distinction between a completed investigation, a selected candidate, a contested result, a collaborative acceptance and independently reproduced agreement.
- Check the live register before resuming. At this edition's checkpoint there are 32 worked first-pass IDs, one separately preserved A-007 revision, and A-033 is next.
- This guide does not create new repository write authority or new blind-test claims. Follow the current stewardship mandate for authorised continuation.

A new GPT can use the guide for informed, guided work. For a genuinely blind experiment, use fresh contexts and the canonical assay/decoder instructions from Fiche Ceathar; neither an atlas-exposed session nor roleplay within it becomes independent by being renamed.

## Case-by-case decision digest

Each Centre and diligence item below is extracted from its recorded investigation. These preserve the specific choices and the questions still needing constraint; they are not new adjudications. The full original reasoning follows in Section 11.

### A-001

> The morning sun reaches mountain villages long before their valleys receive warmth.

**Recorded Centre:** Morning sunlight reaches mountain villages substantially earlier than warmth reaches their own valleys; the completed achievement is that ordered temporal configuration.

**Result and rival:** see the table above and [full record](#case-a-001).

- **Diligence question A-001-D1:** Can an independently frozen record distinguish an established ordered configuration from a relation-constituted temporal status without relying on an extra, noncanonical requirement? Do not assume that Bearing must be causal or require an additional object.
- **Diligence question A-001-D2:** A later blinded assessor should receive the exact source and current protocol, with prior outcomes withheld as far as feasible, and preserve its own pre-lookup record. The protocol's close illustrative example must be disclosed as a possible cue.

### A-002

> A river remembers no borders, yet nations argue over its water.

**Recorded Centre:** The river's indifference to political borders and nations' contestation over its water form a contrast constituted by their opposed treatments of the same resource.

**Result and rival:** see the table above and [full record](#case-a-002).

- **A-002-D1:** independently test whether the full contrast establishes a contextual distinction or a constitutive incongruous bearing. A discriminant must show an actual omission in one complete reading, not merely prefer an axis description.
- **A-002-D2:** audit whether the shared resource is only a common referent or a constitutive focal organiser, using the frozen location rather than noun salience.
- **A-002-D3:** audit the Standing/Bearing distinction without claiming that all contrasts automatically require Bearing. A complete established contrast may expose canonical overlap; preserve any disagreement in a separate revision.

### A-003

> Every forest floor is a crowded marketplace of decay, nourishment, and renewal.

**Recorded Centre:** Every forest floor is characterised as a densely active whole in which decay, nourishment and renewal participate together, expressed through the marketplace metaphor.

**Result and rival:** see the table above and [full record](#case-a-003).

- **A-003-D1:** audit whether the metaphor establishes enough organisation for Integration, rather than only co-presence or connection. The user's integration hypothesis influenced this assay and must remain disclosed.
- **A-003-D2:** audit whether implicit exchange constitutes supporting dependence sufficient for Bearing. Do not impose a noncanonical requirement that the source explicitly detail its mechanism.
- **A-003-D3:** audit shared location versus privileged organising centre without identifying topology from singular grammar.
- **A-003-D4:** an independent future assay should withhold this outcome and the collaborative discussion where feasible; preserve any revision separately.

### A-004

> Somewhere tonight, a child will learn the moon follows no one home.

**Recorded Centre:** Somewhere in the source's tonight, an unspecified child will come to know that the moon follows nobody home; the projected achievement is acquisition of that determinate non-following knowledge.

**Result and rival:** see the table above and [full record](#case-a-004).

- **A-004-D1:** test Differentiation versus Opening for a fully specified knowledge acquisition without classifying learning generically. A discriminant must change the governing achievement rather than rename novelty.
- **A-004-D2:** independently audit outer projected acquisition versus embedded negative relational content. Preserve modality and learner scope in either record.
- **A-004-D3:** audit focal learning locus versus constitutive range of universal content; do not infer either topology from grammar alone.
- **A-004-D4:** avoid reading “will learn” as already realised, or as necessarily correcting a contrary belief. Later context might license those readings, but none was supplied here.

### A-005

> Cities grow by making strangers useful to one another without introductions.

**Recorded Centre:** Cities grow through establishing practical usefulness among strangers without requiring introductions; the retained achievement is that consequential way growth proceeds.

**Result and rival:** see the table above and [full record](#case-a-005).

- **A-005-D1:** discriminate operative useful connections from functional organisation into a city-wide whole. A successful test must show an actual omitted achievement in one complete formulation, not rely on naming a city or demanding an explicit task list.
- **A-005-D2:** audit whether the achieved identity is operative contribution or condition-held usefulness. Do not classify any useful object as Efficacy automatically; retain the specific growth-means content.
- **A-005-D3:** preserve introduction-independence without inferring a former prerequisite lost authority. A later version that adds such a history would be a new Centre, not an interpretation silently inserted here.

### A-006

> The ocean moves heat between continents, quietly shaping weather and harvests.

**Recorded Centre:** The ocean's movement of heat between continents unobtrusively influences weather and harvests; its completed point is that consequential reach through heat transport.

**Result and rival:** see the table above and [full record](#case-a-006).

- **A-006-D1:** independently test whether sufficiency resides in the transport's constitutive span or in the ocean as a privileged source. Neither singular grammar nor spatial extent alone settles topology.
- **A-006-D2:** test operative consequence versus condition-held dependence without treating any physical motion as Efficacy automatically.
- **A-006-D3:** retain the absence of a stated feedback loop, beneficial outcome or integrated-regulation criterion. Do not infer them from a cell name or external scientific knowledge.

### A-007 — current revision 02

> A single seed carries instructions for negotiating soil, sunlight, insects, and time.

**Recorded Centre:** Instructions for negotiating soil, sunlight, insects and time together constitute a single seed's unified instructional endowment.

**Result and rival:** see the table above and [full record](#case-a-007-revision-02).

Revision 02's six learned-correction checks, rival tests and complete reasons are preserved in the full record. Its change is from the old carrier/content premise to an articulated instructional whole, not a reclassification of an unchanged Centre.

### A-008

> When the bridge reopened, two neighborhoods became neighbors again before sunset.

**Recorded Centre:** On the occasion of the bridge reopening, the two neighbourhoods regain their neighbouring connection before sunset; the achieved status is constituted by their relation to one another.

**Result and rival:** see the table above and [full record](#case-a-008).

- **A-008-D1:** audit whether restored neighbour status is genuinely relation-constituted or an established configuration retained as such; no deletion-only proof.
- **A-008-D2:** independently test temporal occasion versus removal of a governing obstacle. A later causal reading requires a separately frozen Centre, not a silent amendment.
- **A-008-D3:** determine whether regained neighbouring connection itself establishes an operative accessibility, without inventing a reported crossing or treating all capacity as requiring execution.

### A-009

> We built clocks to measure time, then arranged our lives around them.

**Recorded Centre:** People construct clocks for measuring time, then organise their lives around those same instruments; the achieved development is the instruments' consequential bearing on their makers' life organisation.

**Result and rival:** see the table above and [full record](#case-a-009).

- **A-009-D1:** independently test actual conduct organisation as operative result versus relation-constituted organisation. Avoid treating every dependency as Bearing or every described action as Efficacy.
- **A-009-D2:** test the whole two-direction development versus a focal instrument's organising power, preserving both stages in any alternative.
- **A-009-D3:** preserve absence of coercion, moral appraisal, displaced prior governor and repeated feedback.

### A-010

> A grandmother's recipe survives because somebody remembers what cannot be written down.

**Recorded Centre:** A grandmother's recipe remains available through somebody's memory of knowledge that cannot be written down.

**Result and rival:** see the table above and [full record](#case-a-010).

- **A-010-D1:** audit preservation anchored by one support versus a genuinely distributed transmission field, without inventing generations.
- **A-010-D2:** test supported availability against accomplished operative capacity while retaining memory's actual contribution. Do not deny Efficacy merely because nothing was cooked.
- **A-010-D3:** retain unwritability without assuming an existing written component or writing's former authority.

### A-011

> The desert is not empty; it simply makes every encounter more consequential.

**Recorded Centre:** **Confirmed Operative Centre:** **The description of the desert as empty is corrected through its making every encounter more consequential.**

**Result and rival:** see the table above and [full record](#case-a-011).

- **A-011-D1:** On independent review, does the frozen holding statement establish constitutive adequacy rather than merely justification for a corrected result? Test 35 against 34 without reopening the source.
- **A-011-D2:** Does the explicit displacement in the frozen record suffice for Release rather than ordinary local correction? Test 35 against 29.

### A-012

> Across the tundra, migrating animals follow routes older than any human map.

**Recorded Centre:** **Confirmed Operative Centre:** **The routes followed by migrating animals across the tundra predate every human map.**

**Result and rival:** see the table above and [full record](#case-a-012).

- **A-012-D2:** Test the recorded across-comparison locus against a privileged relational anchor, using 22 versus 19.

### A-013

> The first question in a classroom can change what everyone notices afterward.

**Recorded Centre:** **Confirmed Operative Centre:** **The first classroom question has the potential to redirect what everyone notices afterward.**

**Result and rival:** see the table above and [full record](#case-a-013).

- **A-013-D1:** Independently test whether an explicitly established potential influence warrants Efficacy without claiming actual exercise. Preserve the modality throughout.
- **A-013-D2:** Confirm that broad recipient scope specifies reach rather than constituting distributed completion; test 21 against 24 from the frozen record.

### A-014

> Even broken pottery tells archaeologists how ordinary hands once held their world.

**Recorded Centre:** **Confirmed Operative Centre:** **Even in broken condition, pottery bears evidence for archaeologists of ordinary people's past practical engagement with their world.**

**Result and rival:** see the table above and [full record](#case-a-014).

- **A-014-D1:** Test 20 against 19: is the indicated past account genuinely source-constituted rather than an established evidence connection? Preserve the positive holding statement; necessity of terms alone is insufficient.
- **A-014-D2:** Test Relating against Opening without supplying an access restriction not present in the frozen record. Do not treat informative subject matter as automatically establishing an operative capacity.

### A-015

> Listen closely: a crowded station contains hundreds of journeys sharing one platform.

**Recorded Centre:** **Confirmed Operative Centre:** **Attention is invited to the crowded station as hundreds of distinct journeys gathered at one shared platform.**

**Result and rival:** see the table above and [full record](#case-a-015).

- **A-015-D1:** Independently test whether common membership suffices for the recorded whole, versus mere co-location in 19. Do not add complementary functions to secure Integration.

### A-016

> The night sky offers ancient light to eyes that opened this morning.

**Recorded Centre:** **Confirmed Operative Centre:** **The night sky's offering of ancient light to eyes opened this morning establishes a contrast between remote antiquity and recently opened seeing within their encounter.**

**Result and rival:** see the table above and [full record](#case-a-016).

- **A-016-D1:** Independently test 05 against 23: does contrasted significance govern, or does the connection itself govern? Preserve the full frozen encounter.

### A-017

> A market price gathers weather, labor, distance, scarcity, and desire into one figure.

**Recorded Centre:** **Confirmed Operative Centre:** **Weather, labor, distance, scarcity and desire are gathered into the single expression of a market-price figure.**

### A-018

> Every coastline is a temporary agreement between restless water and resistant land.

**Recorded Centre:** **Confirmed Operative Centre:** **Every coastline is a provisional boundary constituted through the meeting of restless water and resistant land.**

### A-020

> The world's languages divide experience differently while helping people recognize familiar situations.

**Recorded Centre:** **Confirmed Operative Centre:** **Across the world's languages, divergent divisions of experience coexist with helping people recognise familiar situations, distinguishing variation in articulation from loss of recognisability.**

### A-021

**Recorded Centre:** **Confirmed Operative Centre:** **At midnight, emergencies, recoveries, farewells and beginnings are gathered within one hospital under a common roof.**

### A-022

> Clouds do not consult calendars before bringing rain to waiting fields.

**Recorded Centre:** **Confirmed Operative Centre:** **Rain brought by clouds to waiting fields is distinguished from human calendar scheduling: calendar consultation does not govern its arrival.**

- **A-022-D1:** Independently test whether the achieved distinction stands as independence, or its significance remains context-constituted as in 05. Do not decide from term-deletion alone.

## Guard against over-reading the precedents

Several records use phrases such as “no further dependence,” “no coupled system,” or “no additional capacity” to explain a local choice. These are **case-specific arguments**, not new necessary conditions in the grammar. Distributed does not require a feedback loop or mutually coupled system; its definition includes a span, range, sequence or set of cases. Bearing does not require an extra process beyond the result, and may be non-causal. Efficacy does not require a separate later action. Audit such shorthand against the full frozen identity and exact definitions. If an argument needs a stronger rule than the control supplies, preserve that as unresolved diligence rather than silently installing the rule.

The paired identity formulations test content, not whether a GPT can insert “through” into a sentence. A result formulation can contain a constitutive dependency; a constitution formulation can merely restate prerequisites. Neither wording wins automatically.

# 9. Exact working protocol

The repository protocol follows. Relative links are resolved for standalone reading; substantive text is preserved. The title of Gate Two states the goal of uniqueness; its actual instructions explicitly preserve unresolved survivors.

# Operative Centre Confirmation and the One-Way Threshold

**Working resolution protocol — 20 October 2026.** This procedure supplements the workbook. The canonical definitions and 54-cell coordinates in [THE_54_CONTROL.md](https://github.com/TitanicParker/everything-fresh/blob/main/THE_54_CONTROL.md) remain unchanged.

**Governing direction:** The sentence determines the Operative Centre. The confirmed Centre determines the cell. A proposed cell never determines the Centre retrospectively.

## Gate One — Identify and confirm the Operative Centre

Work with the source sentence **without consulting cell names, the cell lookup, or anticipated coordinates**.

1. **Source boundary.** Record the exact sentence, corpus ID and version. Preserve qualifications, modality, negation, scope and essential dependencies.
2. **Unfolding.** Trace what remains unresolved, how the sentence's contributions develop, and what governing determination makes the entire unfolding sufficient.
3. **Candidate Centre.** State the *smallest complete governing determination*, not a subject summary, theme or attractive paraphrase.
4. **Verification.** Test deletion, alteration, narrowing and reversal of indispensable contributions. Ask whether the proposed Centre still accounts for the original completed achievement. Remove invented causal claims, purposes or implications.
5. **Structural determinacy.** Answer the six questions below **in ordinary, pre-taxonomic language**, as features of the same achieved intelligibility.

| Confirmation question | Required determination |
|---|---|
| What was unresolved? | The specific demand that completion overcomes |
| What changed? | The determination achieved by the whole utterance |
| What made it sufficient? | The indispensable constitutive organisation |
| Where is sufficiency located? | The locus or combination necessary for completion |
| What remains achieved? | The exact identity of what is left achieved, including what must remain constitutive for it to hold as *this* completion (see the holding test below) |
| What would invalidate it? | The smallest change that defeats that achievement |

### Sharpened holding test — complete before freezing

The question **“What remains achieved?”** must do more than name the outcome or say that it continues. While the sentence is still open, determine **what the achievement consists in once it is complete**. Write the answer without canonical axis terms or cell names.

Answer these four prompts as a single description of the Centre:

1. **Identity:** What exactly has been established? Distinguish a completed determination, an achievement constituted by its dependence on something else, and an achieved way something can proceed or operate — *in ordinary language, without selecting among taxonomic categories*.
2. **Constitutive dependency:** If a stated condition, contrast, support, or relation were no longer part of the achievement, would the *identity of the completed result* change, or would only its description or evidential support change? Say precisely which.
3. **Operative continuation:** Does the achieved result itself determine what can subsequently happen, how something can operate, or how a continuation proceeds? If yes, describe the exact consequence established. **Do not infer a capacity merely because a statement is a rule, could be applied, or is practically useful.**
4. **Minimal invalidation:** What smallest change would make the *completed achievement itself* no longer hold? Distinguish loss of the result from loss of the relation constituting it or loss of its operative consequence.

### Achieved-identity discrimination — established result or constitutive bearing

**Use this additional test during Gate One, before the source sentence is closed.** It sharpens identification; it does not change the canonical definitions. Do **not** introduce the labels Standing, Bearing, any cell number, or anticipated classification while carrying out the test.

A relationship, condition, comparison, or plurality of indispensable terms can be necessary to *form* a complete determination without making the achieved identity consist in an ongoing determination of one term **through** another. Necessity of the terms alone is not the discriminant.

Write both formulations in ordinary language:

- **Result formulation:** “What has been established is ___.”
- **Constitution formulation:** “What obtains through what is ___.”

Then test each formulation against the *complete governing achievement* rather than the grammatical ease of writing it:

1. **Completion versus constitution:** Does the full achievement consist in the established determination as a result, or is what has been determined itself the constitutive bearing of terms through one another? Specify the exact difference without dropping qualifications.
2. **Identity rather than prerequisites:** Which dependencies are indispensable **to identifying what the completed result is**, rather than merely being prerequisites for establishing or describing it? Do not infer constitutive bearing solely because removing a term also removes the result: that counterfactual defeats many kinds of completion.
3. **Rival formulation test:** Does either formulation omit part of the completed achievement, or does one invent a dependence not established by the source? If both remain adequate, record the exact difference and pursue further pre-freeze tests; do not manufacture uniqueness by paraphrase.
4. **Holding statement:** State positively *what remains achieved* and *why its identity holds that way*, so the frozen record can discriminate an established relational configuration from a determination constituted through the terms' bearing.

**Illustrative discrimination, not a lookup or automatic classification:** An established temporal lead between two arrivals may be achieved *as the ordered configuration itself*, even though both arrivals are required to describe the lead. An exact quantitative determination of one magnitude through specified counterparts may instead have that quantitative dependence as the identity of what is achieved. In either case the source analysis, not the illustration or the words “temporal” and “quantitative,” must decide.

This test **never treats relational syntax as sufficient for constitutive bearing**, and never treats the mere fact that a relationship is established as sufficient to exclude such bearing. If the resulting Centre remains genuinely neutral, preserve that distinction rather than silently rewriting it after confirmation.

**Required final line in Gate One:** “What remains achieved is ___; it holds as this achievement because ___; removing ___ would change/defeat it in the following way: ___.”

The record must distinguish the **constitution of completion** (what makes it sufficient) from the **identity of what remains achieved** (what persists as the completed result). Do not mistake the presence of multiple terms for a distributed completion, a relational sentence for relation-dependent holding, or the description of motion/change for an achieved operative consequence. These distinctions must come from the Centre analysis, not from a preview of the 54 cells.

If two materially different accounts of what remains achieved still fit, continue testing them **before** confirmation. Do not leave the choice to cell selection after the One-Way Threshold.

Before confirming, check **coverage** (nothing governing omitted), **minimality** (nothing unnecessary included), **non-invention** (nothing unsupported added), and **determinacy** (one complete governing achievement described).

### Centre Confirmation Record — required fields

- Sentence ID and source version (provenance only).
- **Confirmed Operative Centre:** one exact, minimally sufficient formulation.
- **Opening constraint:** the unresolvedness.
- **Achieved completion:** what is made sufficient.
- **Constitutive organisation:** how the achievement becomes sufficient.
- **Location of sufficiency:** what is constitutive of its completion.
- **Mode of holding:** the completed holding-test line, specifying the result's identity, what remains constitutive of that identity, and the minimal change that defeats it.
- **Decisive counterfactual:** the smallest variation that would defeat it.
- **Confirmation:** coverage, minimality, determinacy and non-invention check.

The confirmation record must be self-sufficient for cell derivation. Its descriptions must arise from the source analysis, **not** from the vocabulary of the canonical dimensions.

When the record passes Gate One, write exactly:

**OPERATIVE CENTRE CONFIRMED — SOURCE SENTENCE CLOSED.**

## The One-Way Threshold

**After confirmation, do not reread, quote, paraphrase, amend or appeal to the original sentence to decide a dimension or cell.** The classifier works only from the frozen Centre Confirmation Record and the canonical reference.

A dispute between cells is a question about the structure of the **confirmed Centre**, not an invitation to select a phrase from the source sentence to decide between them. Do not retrofit the Centre to match a candidate.

## Gate Two — Determine the unique cell

1. Take the complete frozen Centre Confirmation Record as the sole sentence-specific input.
2. Consult [THE_54_CONTROL.md](https://github.com/TitanicParker/everything-fresh/blob/main/THE_54_CONTROL.md). Determine the unified completion under **simultaneous** Transformation Pattern × Completion Topology × Persistence Mode. The dimensions are not successive operations or independent descriptive labels.
3. Eliminate incompatible cells by reference to **specific constraints of the Centre**, not resemblance to cell names or individual words.
4. Compare the strongest surviving neighbouring cells: which demands a different constitution of sufficiency, governing transformation or mode of holding?
5. Identify the **single surviving cell**, giving number, canonical name, conjunction, and the structural reasons alternatives fail. If more than one survives, specify the exact unresolved structural distinction in the frozen record rather than making a preference-based selection.

**The desired result is a Centre determinate enough to exclude the other 53 candidates.** The one-way threshold ensures the dimensional identity is derived from that Centre alone.

Read this file, [WORKFLOW.md](https://github.com/TitanicParker/everything-fresh/blob/main/WORKFLOW.md), the relevant Operative Centre chapters and the canonical [THE_54_CONTROL.md](https://github.com/TitanicParker/everything-fresh/blob/main/THE_54_CONTROL.md). Report the nearest structural rival and why it fails, using **only** the frozen Centre record after closure.

**Status:** Single-file working master for the first, flat GitHub Pages experiment.

## Purpose and boundaries

This is a **lookup sheet and controlling reference**, not a manifesto, research programme, repository scaffold, or web application. It contains the entire canonical 15-definition vocabulary; the compositional/numbering derivation; the full 54-cell table with coordinate-generated forms, canonical crystallizations and companion utterances; and the complete frozen GPT-D derivation instrument. ## The 15 definitions

## 3.1 The three primitive dimensions

1. **Transformation Pattern** — Specifies what kind of unresolvedness is altered between the opening and completed situation.
2. **Completion Topology** — Specifies the structural shape by which the transformation counts as complete.
3. **Persistence Mode** — Specifies how the achieved completion remains available for subsequent uptake.

## 3.2 Transformation Pattern values

4. **Differentiation** — A difference that was unavailable, flattened, conflated, or non-operative becomes determinate enough to matter.
5. **Opening** — Availability, reach, continuation, access, or possibility becomes greater than it was under the opening constraint.
6. **Relating** — A bearing, dependency, correspondence, ordering, reciprocity, or mutual determination becomes constitutive of the situation.
7. **Repair** — An organization that fails relative to an operative criterion is changed so that the misfit or fault no longer governs the result.
8. **Integration** — Separate parts acquire determinate roles through organization into a whole and thereby gain structure, mutual support, or joint capacity.
9. **Settlement** — A live demand for further uptake, choice, explanation, completion, adjudication, or continuation is discharged.

## 3.3 Completion Topology values

10. **Focal** — The transformation becomes complete because completion gathers around one privileged locus. That locus is constitutive of why the transformation counts as complete.
11. **Distributed** — The transformation becomes complete only across a constitutive plurality, span, sequence, range, reciprocal field, set of cases, or system. No single locus is sufficient by itself.
12. **Release** — The transformation becomes complete because something that previously governed the situation loses its determining force. Release means de-governance, not merely removal, absence, negation, separation, physical movement away, or negative wording.

## 3.4 Persistence Mode values

13. **Standing** — The achieved determination itself remains available as the completed result.
14. **Bearing** — The achieved result remains what it is only through a constitutive relation, dependency, contrast, condition, source, proportion, frame, support, or mutual determination.
15. **Efficacy** — The achieved result remains as an accomplished capacity, operative effect, changed continuation, rule, function, or consequential difference in what can happen.

---

---

## Dimensional Conjunction

**Dimensional Conjunction is not a fourth dimension.**

It is the rule by which Transformation Pattern, Completion Topology, and Persistence Mode mutually determine one cell.

A coordinate is written:

`Transformation Pattern × Completion Topology × Persistence Mode`

The notation locates a position in the grammar. It does **not** mean:

`Transformation characteristic + Topology characteristic + Persistence characteristic`

The three dimensions are simultaneous relational constraints upon **one completion-event**.

At cell level, none of the dimensions contributes an independent detachable property. Instead:

- the **Transformation Pattern** is realized only as that transformation becomes complete through this Topology and exists as achieved in this Persistence Mode;
- the **Completion Topology** is the topology specifically of this Transformation in this Persistence Mode;
- the **Persistence Mode** is the persistence specifically of this Transformation completed through this Topology.

Each dimension therefore constrains how the other two can be realized.

Their conjunction is **reciprocal determination**.

The resulting cell has **one unified characteristic**.

> **The coordinate is a constraint that generates the form, not a description of three properties possessed by the form.**

## 4.1 Non-Cartesian rule

The coordinate space is mathematically indexable as `T × K × P`, but its semantics are not additive or Cartesian.

There is no valid semantic derivation:

`Transformation → Topology → Persistence`

There is no first, second, or third dimension in deriving a cell. All three constraints operate simultaneously.

Do not derive one dimensional result and then modify, qualify, pluralize, release, preserve, relate, or activate it with another dimension.

## 4.2 Non-operator rule

No dimension is an operation performed upon another dimension.

In particular:

- **Focal** does not simply select one already-completed object or distinction.
- **Distributed** does not simply pluralize an already-completed form.
- **Release** does not release, cancel, weaken, undo, or negate the Transformation Pattern.
- **Standing** does not simply preserve an already-completed result.
- **Bearing** does not merely add a relation to an otherwise complete result.
- **Efficacy** does not merely add usefulness, later action, or an external consequence.

A coordinate must remain fully a form of its Transformation Pattern regardless of Topology or Persistence Mode.

What counts as the focal locus, distributed field, released governance, standing determination, constitutive bearing, or efficacy must be resolved **within the unity of that cell**.

## 4.3 Relational-field rule

A cell is constrained partly by what neighboring cells cannot be.

Changing one coordinate dimension while holding the other two fixed should change the identity of the completion, not merely substitute predictable vocabulary.

Neighboring coordinates may therefore be used as **negative constraints** during derivation, but they must not become templates from which answers are generated by analogy.

The grammar may be regular in its constraints without being verbally regular in its crystallizations.

---

---

## Cell-number derivation

Let:

- **T** = {Differentiation, Opening, Relating, Repair, Integration, Settlement}
- **K** = {Focal, Distributed, Release}
- **P** = {Standing, Bearing, Efficacy}

A coordinate is:

`c = <t, k, p> ∈ T × K × P`

Therefore:

`|T × K × P| = 6 × 3 × 3 = 54`

The product notation and numbering index the space. They do not imply additive cell semantics.

Ordering rule:

- Persistence Mode changes every cell: Standing → Bearing → Efficacy.
- Completion Topology changes every three cells: Focal → Distributed → Release.
- Transformation Pattern changes every nine cells: Differentiation → Opening → Relating → Repair → Integration → Settlement.

With zero-based indices `i`, `j`, `k`:

`cell = 9i + 3j + k + 1`

## Gate One — source analysis and Centre confirmation

### Source boundary and unfolding

Exact frozen source (Corpus A, §15, item 1):

> The morning sun reaches mountain villages long before their valleys receive warmth.

The source is an ordinary general statement. Its generic plural is retained without adding a universal claim about every village or a measurement of the interval. Sunlight reaching villages and warmth reaching their own valleys are different specified arrivals. No account of terrain, temperature, thermal causation or the sun's physical mechanism is supplied.

1. “The morning sun” establishes the morning setting and source.
2. “reaches mountain villages” establishes an arrival, leaving its comparison with the valleys unresolved.
3. “long before” introduces substantial precedence, requiring the later endpoint.
4. “their valleys receive warmth” supplies that endpoint and the village–own-valley pairing. The whole establishes the substantial ordered separation.

### Candidate Centres and eliminations

| Candidate | Assessment |
|---|---|
| Morning sunlight reaches mountain villages. | Incomplete: the governing timing comparison and valley endpoint are absent. |
| Villages are warm while valleys are cold. | Unsupported: exchanges sunlight/warmth arrivals for absolute temperature states. |
| Morning sunlight reaches the villages substantially earlier than warmth reaches their own valleys. | Complete: preserves the two arrivals, pairing, direction and substantial interval. |
| Terrain delays the warming of valleys. | Invents a causal explanation absent from the utterance. |
| The village arrival gains a dependent status of earliness through the valley arrival. | Must be tested: it may merely redescribe a position in the completed ordering rather than identify an additional achieved status. |

### Deletion, alteration, narrowing, reversal and dependency tests

| Test | Variation and exact consequence |
|---|---|
| Deletion of comparison | Retain only the first arrival: an arrival report survives, but no substantial lead. Survival of a grammatical clause does not establish its priority over the original whole. |
| Deletion of degree | Remove “long”: precedence survives but the substantially earlier determination is lost. |
| Alteration of endpoint | Replace valley warmth with valley illumination: the two arrivals being compared change. Their difference must be retained. |
| Narrowing to one contribution | Retain only the valley endpoint or only the village endpoint: neither establishes their lead. |
| Scope narrowing | Replace the generic plural with one named instance: the particular relation can survive, but the source's generic scope is narrowed. |
| Reversal | Replace precedence with its opposite: both arrivals can remain, but this ordering is defeated. |
| Minimal order invalidation | Make the two arrivals simultaneous: both occurrences remain, while their lead disappears. |
| Dependency | Both arrivals and their separation are necessary to identify the order. No occurrence is asserted to cause, sustain or operate through the other. Deleting a necessary argument defeats many results and does not decide how this one holds. |
| Sufficiency-location | No arrival alone, connective alone or prominent subject alone is enough. Their paired substantial ordering is sufficient; no further mechanism is needed. |

### Four sharpened holding tests

1. **Identity.** What remains achieved is the substantial temporal ordering itself, including the specified arrivals and their pairing. It is neither an isolated arrival nor an independent capacity.
2. **Constitutive dependencies.** Change the endpoints, direction or substantial interval and the identity of this ordering changes. These are the content of the ordered configuration. That necessity does not by itself establish that the identity of either occurrence is produced or sustained through the other.
3. **Operative continuation.** No changed continuation, practical capacity or prescribed way of proceeding is established. Nor is a causal heating relationship asserted.
4. **Minimal invalidation.** Simultaneity eliminates precedence while leaving both arrivals intact. Removing substantial separation alone defeats the specifically substantial lead but preserves a weaker ordering. The first defeats the order; the second changes its degree.

### Achieved-identity discrimination

**Result formulation:** What has been established is a substantial temporal ordering of morning sunlight at mountain villages before warmth in their own valleys.

**Constitution formulation:** What obtains through what is the village arrival's temporal priority relative to the later arrival of valley warmth.

**Completion versus constitution.** The latter names a position within the completed ordering. The complete result formulation already retains that priority. Nothing further is lost when the whole achievement is described as the ordered configuration itself.

**Identity rather than prerequisites.** The paired endpoints, morning setting, direction and interval specify which order has been established. Their indispensability is acknowledged; it is not used as an automatic argument that a further dependent identity has been accomplished.

**Further pre-freeze tests.**

- Retain both occurrences but erase their order: the achievement is lost.
- Retain the full substantial order but omit a separate statement about “acquired earliness”: nothing governing is lost; that statement duplicates a position in the order.
- Change either timing: the configuration changes. No separately established function, prominence, authority or capacity is shown to change through it.
- Deny that one arrival causes the other while preserving their timing: the achievement survives; a causal dependence is unnecessary.

**Discrimination outcome.** The complete ordered configuration is the retained achievement. The “through” formulation is adequate as a redescription of its positions, but supplies no independently supported difference in achieved identity. This does not require that every dependence be causal; the test concerns whether a different constitutive achievement has actually been identified. Residual interpretive limits must be reported, not hidden by paraphrase.

### Frozen Centre Confirmation Record

| Required field / question | Frozen determination |
|---|---|
| Sentence ID and source version | A-001; Corpus A §15, Edition 1.1 workbook; source file `One_Hundred_Statements_About_the_World(4).md`; workbook blob `1b511a439a5007a3c4a61c36ff5417f415005e2c`. |
| Confirmed Operative Centre | Morning sunlight reaches mountain villages substantially earlier than warmth reaches their own valleys; the completed achievement is that ordered temporal configuration. |
| 1. What was unresolved? / Opening constraint | The relative timing of the two specified arrivals. |
| 2. What changed? / Achieved completion | A substantial village-before-valley lead is determined, with the generic scope and morning setting retained. |
| 3. What made it sufficient? / Constitutive organisation | The village/own-valley pairing, distinct arrivals, direction of precedence and substantial interval jointly specify the lead. |
| 4. Where is sufficiency located? | Across both arrivals and their substantial temporal separation. No isolated endpoint, grammatical subject or connective suffices. |
| 5. What remains achieved? / Identity | The complete ordering remains as the result. Its endpoints and interval belong to its content; no further term-status or operative consequence beyond that configuration is established. |
| 6. What would invalidate it? / Decisive counterfactual | Simultaneous arrivals defeat precedence while preserving both occurrences. Loss of substantial separation changes the specifically substantial lead into a weaker result. |
| Confirmation: coverage | Morning, village/own-valley pairing, sunlight/warmth distinction, substantial interval, direction and generic scope are retained. |
| Confirmation: minimality | No explanation of terrain, absolute temperature, heating mechanism or practical application is required. |
| Confirmation: non-invention | No causal production, changed capacity or universal quantifier is added. |
| Confirmation: determinacy | One governing achievement is recorded: the full substantial ordering. The alternative “through” phrasing adds no demonstrated difference beyond its positions. This is a substantive interpretive determination, not proof of exhaustive taxonomic uniqueness. |

**Mode of holding — frozen statement**

What remains achieved is the substantial temporal ordering of morning sunlight at mountain villages before warmth in those villages' valleys; it holds as this achievement because the complete ordered configuration has been determined, with its specified endpoints, pairing and interval retained as its content; removing precedence would defeat it in the following way: simultaneous arrivals would preserve both occurrences but eliminate the established lead.
