# A-017 — first-pass resolution and diligence

**Record ID:** FRESHER-A-017-2026-10-21-01. **Date / assessor:** 21 October 2026, Europe/Dublin; Fresher / OpenAI Codex.
**Source:** frozen Corpus A §15, Edition 1.1, blob `1b511a439a5007a3c4a61c36ff5417f415005e2c`. **Protocol:** achieved-identity confirmation, blob `69eb1e465b48bbc2e0fef0d315456a6ebabd2107`; workflow blob `f522e66fd3b068e0b07cee16c8e21ee99121ebeb`. A-016 was saved, indexed and published before this investigation.

## Gate One — source analysis

> A market price gathers weather, labor, distance, scarcity, and desire into one figure.

### Unfolding and candidates

A market price is identified. “Gathers” makes the following plurality belong together in a result. Weather, labor, distance, scarcity and desire supply distinct contributions. “Into one figure” specifies the unity accomplished.

1. Prices are affected by several things. Preserves influence but omits gathering into one figure.
2. The five factors have equal numerical weights in a price formula. Invents equality and a formula.
3. A single market-price figure gathers weather, labor, distance, scarcity and desire into one expression. Preserves plurality and their unified expression.
4. Price determines everyone's conduct. Invents consequential behaviour.

Candidate 3 is strongest. No exhaustive theory of price, exact weighting, efficient-market claim or guaranteed fairness is supplied.

### Diagnostic tests

| Test | Finding |
|---|---|
| Deletion | Remove the factors and the price loses its specified composite character. Remove “one figure” and a range of influences remains without the explicit unified expression. |
| Alteration | Make one factor alone determine price: replaces gathering with single-source determination. Replace gathering with listing: factors remain separate. |
| Narrowing | Retain only weather and labor: changes the stated range. |
| Reversal | One figure splitting into five independent accounts reverses the direction of organisation. |
| Dependency | The figure expresses the combined contributions; the sentence does not give a continuously recalculated formula or supporting equilibrium. |
| Sufficiency-location | The one figure is constitutive as the common expression in which the plurality gathers. |
| Minimal invalidation | The five influences remain separately listed with no unified price figure: the gathering achievement fails. |

### Six questions

| Question | Answer |
|---|---|
| What was unresolved? | How the disparate factors belong to the market price. |
| What changed? | They are gathered into one price figure. |
| What made it sufficient? | A plurality of specified contributions receives a single expression. |
| Where is sufficiency located? | In the one market-price figure as common expression. |
| What remains achieved? | A unified price expression incorporating the stated contributions. |
| What would invalidate it? | Leaving the factors separate without that unified figure. |

### Four holding tests

1. **Identity:** a composite single expression, not a list, formula or newly enabled trade.
2. **Dependencies:** the named contributions constitute its particular composite character; removing one changes that character. Their necessity does not establish a further sustaining mechanism.
3. **Operative continuation:** no completed purchase, allocation, decision or trading capacity is established. Practical use of prices is not imported.
4. **Minimal invalidation:** retain all named factors but eliminate their common expression; the unified achievement disappears.

### Achieved-identity discrimination

**What has been established is:** one market-price figure as the gathered expression of the named contributions.

**What obtains through what is:** their combined expression obtains in the single figure.

The latter states the organisation of the result. It does not establish that a separate support must continually maintain the figure's coherence, or that the record's main achievement is a conditional price law. The completed identity is the unified expression rather than a mechanism of its ongoing adjustment.

### Frozen Centre Confirmation Record

**Confirmed Operative Centre:** **Weather, labor, distance, scarcity and desire are gathered into the single expression of a market-price figure.**

**Opening constraint:** the disparate contributions' common expression is unspecified.
**Achieved completion:** they constitute one composite price expression.
**Constitutive organisation:** the named plurality converges into a single figure without an asserted weighting formula.
**Location of sufficiency:** the figure as the privileged common expression.
**Mode of holding:** **What remains achieved is one price figure as the gathered expression of weather, labor, distance, scarcity and desire; it holds as this achievement because the plurality is organised into that unified expression; removing their common figure would leave separate influences without this completed gathering.**
**Decisive counterfactual:** all factors remain listed but no single price expression gathers them.
**Confirmation:** preserves all five contributions and the single result; adds no equal weighting, exhaustiveness, transaction or mechanism; identifies one composite expression.

**OPERATIVE CENTRE CONFIRMED — SOURCE SENTENCE CLOSED.**

## Gate Two — frozen record and canonical reference only

**Canonical authority:** THE_54_CONTROL.md, blob `113cdf0c50a2eb8645189f0505fa8a9584028322`.

### Unified conjunction

**37 — Focal whole; Integration × Focal × Standing.**

Disparate contributions acquire common organisation in one expression; that expression constitutes their completed unity and remains as the result.

### Alternatives

| Dimension | Assessment |
|---|---|
| Transformation | Integration fits separate contributions composed into a unified expression. Relating would retain multiple influences but omit their completed gathering. Differentiation no governing distinction; Opening no enlarged availability; Repair no criterion failure; Settlement no live choice or adjudication discharged. |
| Topology | Focal fits the figure constitutive of why the contributions count as unified. Distributed would foreground the array of roles across a structure rather than their convergence in one common expression. Release has no former governance losing force. |
| Persistence | Standing fits the composite expression left achieved. Bearing would require coherence held through an indispensable sustaining anchor or constitutive support rather than the completed organisation itself. Efficacy would infer price-directed behaviour absent from the record. |

### Strongest rivals

**40 — Role array; Integration × Distributed × Standing.** Retains composition but changes where completion is sufficient. The record identifies the one figure as the common expression; it does not articulate complementary positions across a dispersed whole. Many factors alone do not establish Distributed completion.

**38 — Frame-held whole; Integration × Focal × Bearing.** Would take coherence through an anchor as the identity. The frozen record instead retains the unified expression itself; no additional supporting framework is identified.

**20 — Pivoted balance; Relating × Focal × Bearing.** A conditional value through factors could support this, but the frozen achievement is gathering their contributions into a common expression, not determining an unknown magnitude through a specified dependency law.

“Focal whole” describes unity at the single expression; it does not claim all economic reality is exhausted by price.

### Diligence

**Status:** candidate 37. **Confidence:** medium-high in the governing gathering, moderate in persistence. **Uniqueness:** selected under this frozen record; independent reproducibility not established.
- **A-017-D1:** Test the focal-expression account against a distributed contribution array without equating a singular figure with Focal by grammar alone.
- **A-017-D2:** Test the completed composite expression against a factor-constituted value; do not add an ongoing pricing law absent from the record.

No earlier record or revision. Next target: A-018.

# A-018 — first-pass resolution and diligence

**Record ID:** FRESHER-A-018-2026-10-21-01. **Date / assessor:** 21 October 2026, Europe/Dublin; Fresher / OpenAI Codex.
**Source:** frozen Corpus A §15, Edition 1.1, blob `1b511a439a5007a3c4a61c36ff5417f415005e2c`. **Protocol:** achieved-identity confirmation, blob `69eb1e465b48bbc2e0fef0d315456a6ebabd2107`; workflow blob `f522e66fd3b068e0b07cee16c8e21ee99121ebeb`. A-017 was fully saved, indexed and published before this investigation.

## Gate One — source analysis

> Every coastline is a temporary agreement between restless water and resistant land.

### Unfolding and candidates

“Every coastline” identifies the scope. “Temporary agreement” characterises each boundary as provisional, rather than fixed independently. “Between restless water and resistant land” specifies its counterpart conditions. “Agreement” is figurative; no consciousness, contract or consent is attributed to water and land.

1. Coastlines exist where land meets water. Loses provisionality and the contrasting contributions.
2. Water defeats land and moves the coastline inland. Adds a direction and an outcome.
3. Every coastline is a provisional boundary constituted through restless water meeting resistant land. Preserves the temporary arrangement and both counterparts.
4. Water and land form a self-maintaining whole. Adds systemic stability and self-maintenance.

Candidate 3 survives. “Temporary” does not supply a duration or a prediction of a particular future movement.

### Diagnostic tests

| Test | Finding |
|---|---|
| Deletion | Remove either water or land: the specified between-boundary disappears. Remove temporary: provisionality is lost. |
| Alteration | Replace temporary agreement with permanent inscription: an independently fixed boundary replaces the contingent arrangement. |
| Narrowing | “Some coastlines” weakens the universal scope. |
| Reversal | Make land restless and water resistant: changes the roles within the provisional relation, rather than merely reordering the nouns. |
| Dependency | The coastline is this boundary through the meeting of the two counterparts; neither alone constitutes it. This goes beyond evidence for an independently constituted line. |
| Sufficiency-location | Completion lies across the meeting of water and land, not in an independent marker or one side's activity alone. |
| Operative restraint | No rate, net movement, equilibrium mechanism or erosion history is specified. |
| Minimal invalidation | Remove the water–land meeting: a line could remain drawn, but it would not be this achieved provisional boundary. |

### Six questions

| Question | Answer |
|---|---|
| What was unresolved? | What kind of boundary a coastline is and what constitutes it. |
| What changed? | Each coastline is determined as provisional through the water–land encounter. |
| What made it sufficient? | Restless water and resistant land are given counterpart roles in the temporary arrangement. |
| Where is sufficiency located? | Across their meeting; neither counterpart alone establishes the boundary. |
| What remains achieved? | A provisional boundary whose identity obtains through that opposed meeting. |
| What would invalidate it? | Remove the meeting or make the boundary independent of it. |

### Four holding tests

1. **Identity:** a provisional water–land boundary, not a permanent coordinate or a literal contract.
2. **Constitutive dependencies:** water and land are constituents of the boundary's identity, not merely causes or evidence encountered earlier.
3. **Operative continuation:** the result is characterised as temporary, but no specific changed course, movement or accomplished control is established.
4. **Minimal invalidation:** leave a mapped line while removing the opposed meeting; the geographical boundary described is no longer constituted there.

### Achieved-identity discrimination

**What has been established is:** every coastline is a provisional arrangement between water and land.

**What obtains through what is:** the coastline's provisional boundary obtains through restless water meeting resistant land.

The second explains the identity of the boundary, rather than merely supplying a reason to believe a line exists. A detached line can remain drawn after its boundary relation changes; it would no longer be the same constituted coastline. The achievement does not require literal equality of forces or stable equilibrium.

### Frozen Centre Confirmation Record

**Confirmed Operative Centre:** **Every coastline is a provisional boundary constituted through the meeting of restless water and resistant land.**

**Opening constraint:** the coastline's boundary character and constitution are unspecified.
**Achieved completion:** the boundary is understood as provisional through its two opposed counterparts.
**Constitutive organisation:** water's restlessness and land's resistance define their meeting as a temporary arrangement.
**Location of sufficiency:** across the meeting of both counterparts.
**Mode of holding:** **What remains achieved is each coastline's provisional boundary; it holds as this achievement because restless water and resistant land constitute it through their meeting; removing that opposed meeting would leave any retained line without this coastline's constituted identity.**
**Decisive counterfactual:** an independently fixed line remains despite the water–land meeting no longer constituting it.
**Confirmation:** preserves every, provisionality and counterpart qualities; adds no intentional agreement, movement direction or stabilising mechanism; identifies one constituted boundary.

**OPERATIVE CENTRE CONFIRMED — SOURCE SENTENCE CLOSED.**

## Gate Two — frozen record and canonical reference only

**Canonical authority:** THE_54_CONTROL.md, blob `113cdf0c50a2eb8645189f0505fa8a9584028322`.

### Unified conjunction

**23 — Reciprocal shift; Relating × Distributed × Bearing.**

The boundary is determinate across the opposed counterparts and exists as that boundary through their meeting. The same relation specifies the transformation, why it is complete and how the result obtains.

### Alternatives

| Dimension | Assessment |
|---|---|
| Transformation | Relating fits a boundary constituted between counterparts. Differentiation would retain separation but omit how its boundary obtains. Integration would add organisation into a coherent whole; Opening adds access; Repair adds a failed criterion; Settlement turns provisional arrangement into discharged demand without warrant. |
| Topology | Distributed: the record explicitly requires both counterparts' meeting, with neither independent locus sufficient. Focal would make an independent anchor or boundary marker govern, contrary to the record's across-meeting locus. Release has no prior governance displaced. |
| Persistence | Bearing: the boundary exists through the counterpart relation. Standing would leave an established line while omitting its constituted identity. Efficacy would add a particular operative movement, control or changed continuation. |

### Strongest rivals

**20 — Pivoted balance; Relating × Focal × Bearing.** Strongest topology rival because the boundary could be treated as a focal term dependent on counterparts. But the frozen record explicitly locates sufficiency across their meeting and denies an independently sufficient locus. Merely identifying one boundary does not establish Focal.

**22 — Sequential link; Relating × Distributed × Standing.** Would retain a determined arrangement. Here the identity of the boundary is constituted by its actual counterpart relation: a drawn arrangement can survive while no longer being this boundary.

**44 — Sustained equilibrium; Integration × Release × Bearing.** The name may resemble a temporary balance, but the record establishes neither integrated equilibrium nor displaced inherited divisions. Name resemblance cannot supply those structures.

“Reciprocal shift” names the constituted relationship. No actual migration of the boundary or symmetric physical change is required by this record.

### Diligence

**Status:** candidate 23. **Confidence:** medium-high in constitutive relation; moderate in topology. **Uniqueness:** selected under the frozen record; independent reproducibility not established.
- **A-018-D1:** Test 23 against 20: does completion really lie across counterparts, or at the constituted boundary as privileged locus? Use the frozen locus account, not singular grammar.
- **A-018-D2:** Preserve provisionality without importing dynamic equilibrium, actual displacement or a release of governance.

No earlier record or revision. Next target: A-019.

# A-019 — first-pass resolution and diligence

**Record ID:** FRESHER-A-019-2026-10-21-01. **Date / assessor:** 21 October 2026, Europe/Dublin; Fresher / OpenAI Codex.
**Source:** frozen Corpus A §15, Edition 1.1, blob `1b511a439a5007a3c4a61c36ff5417f415005e2c`. **Protocol:** achieved-identity confirmation, blob `69eb1e465b48bbc2e0fef0d315456a6ebabd2107`; workflow blob `f522e66fd3b068e0b07cee16c8e21ee99121ebeb`. A-018 was fully saved, indexed and published before this investigation.

## Gate One — source analysis

> Someone repairing a bicycle restores more than motion; they restore tomorrow's possibilities.

### Unfolding and candidates

Someone is repairing a bicycle. “Restores more than motion” says physical movement alone is an inadequate measure of what is recovered. “They restore tomorrow's possibilities” completes the practical significance: usable mobility returns as renewed future options.

“Restore” preserves recovery rather than wholly unprecedented creation. “Possibilities” does not assert tomorrow's actual journeys. No particular owner, fault, destination or successful future trip is specified.

1. A bicycle moves again. Incomplete: omits the practical scope of the restoration.
2. Repair creates entirely new future opportunities. Overstates novelty and loses restoration.
3. Repairing the bicycle restores motion together with the future possibilities that recovered mobility makes available. Preserves repair and its expanded significance.
4. A person will definitely travel to work tomorrow. Invents destination, beneficiary and actual performance.

Candidate 3 is strongest. The restoration is one practical recovery, not two unrelated benefits.

### Diagnostic tests

| Test | Finding |
|---|---|
| Deletion | Remove tomorrow's possibilities: mechanical restoration remains but its completed practical significance is lost. Remove repair and motion: a general claim of future options lacks the particular recovery through bicycle repair. |
| Alteration | “Creates” replaces recovery with novelty. “Guarantees tomorrow's journeys” replaces possibilities with asserted performance. |
| Narrowing | Reduce restoration to a turning wheel while future use remains unavailable: fails the sentence's expanded practical achievement. |
| Reversal | Make tomorrow's possibilities repair the bicycle: reverses the relation between restoration and its reach. |
| Dependency | Future possibilities are recovered through restored usable mobility. The sentence does not make the person's entire future depend exclusively on this bicycle. |
| Sufficiency-location | The bicycle's restored functioning is the decisive point through which renewed possibilities are made intelligible; no distributed repair process or social system is described. |
| Criterion | A repair implies a condition requiring remedy. The stated criterion extends beyond bare movement to recovered practical availability. No specific mechanical defect is invented. |
| Minimal invalidation | The bicycle can move mechanically, but its repair restores no practical future possibilities: physical repair remains while the complete asserted recovery fails. |

### Six questions

| Question | Answer |
|---|---|
| What was unresolved? | What the bicycle repair restores beyond physical motion. |
| What changed? | Its restored functioning is understood as renewed possibilities for tomorrow. |
| What made it sufficient? | Recovered motion is connected to its practical future availability rather than treated as an isolated mechanical result. |
| Where is sufficiency located? | At restored bicycle functioning as the particular enabling recovery. |
| What remains achieved? | Recovered usable mobility with its renewed future possibilities. |
| What would invalidate it? | Mechanical motion returns without the practical availability the completed restoration includes. |

### Four holding tests

1. **Identity:** a practical recovery of mobility, including future possibilities; not just a repaired object or a promised actual journey.
2. **Dependencies:** the restored options belong to recovered functioning. Merely remembering a repair or possessing an inert bicycle does not preserve this recovered availability.
3. **Operative continuation:** yes; possibilities for tomorrow are expressly restored. Their availability is achieved while their exercise is not asserted.
4. **Minimal invalidation:** retain mechanical motion while deny recovered practical possibilities; the expanded restoration is no longer achieved.

### Achieved-identity discrimination

**What has been established is:** bicycle repair restores usable motion and thereby renews future possibilities.

**What obtains through what is:** renewed practical possibilities obtain through the bicycle's recovered functioning.

The dependence belongs to the account, but the governing retained achievement is the recovered ability to proceed. A detached repaired condition without available continuation omits the expressly expanded restoration. No future journey must actually occur.

### Frozen Centre Confirmation Record

**Confirmed Operative Centre:** **Repairing a bicycle restores usable motion as renewed practical possibilities for tomorrow, rather than physical movement alone.**

**Opening constraint:** the practical extent of what bicycle repair restores is undetermined.
**Achieved completion:** restored functioning counts as recovery of usable mobility with future possibilities.
**Constitutive organisation:** the remedied bicycle's motion is understood through the practical continuation it makes available again.
**Location of sufficiency:** restored bicycle functioning as the particular enabling recovery.
**Mode of holding:** **What remains achieved is recovered usable mobility with renewed possibilities for tomorrow; it holds as this achievement because restored bicycle functioning makes that practical continuation available again; removing renewed practical availability would leave mechanical movement without the full recovery established.**
**Decisive counterfactual:** mechanical movement returns but no practical future possibility is restored.
**Confirmation:** preserves repair, motion, restored rather than novel possibilities, and tomorrow; adds no named defect, beneficiary, destination or actual journey; identifies one practical recovery.

**OPERATIVE CENTRE CONFIRMED — SOURCE SENTENCE CLOSED.**

## Gate Two — frozen record and canonical reference only

**Canonical authority:** THE_54_CONTROL.md, blob `113cdf0c50a2eb8645189f0505fa8a9584028322`.

### Unified conjunction

**30 — Load transfer; Repair × Focal × Efficacy.**

The particular enabling function is restored to practical adequacy, and that correction remains as recovered capacity for continuation. Physical movement alone does not satisfy the recorded criterion.

### Alternatives

| Dimension | Assessment |
|---|---|
| Transformation | Repair fits restored functioning measured by recovered practical availability. Opening is the strongest rival because possibilities return, but the frozen achievement is expressly recovery through remedy of the enabling function. Relating would retain enablement while omitting recovery; Differentiation would retain “more than” without the restoration; Integration no organised whole; Settlement no final choice or adjudication. |
| Topology | Focal: the particular enabling function is the decisive point of recovery. Distributed would need restoration across a constitutive process, field or stages absent from the record. Release would require a governing constraint losing authority as completion's structure; correction of a fault alone does not establish that. |
| Persistence | Efficacy: recovered possibilities are part of what is achieved, not an incidental use imagined afterward. Standing would leave a repaired condition without recovered availability. Bearing would leave support-dependent options but omit the achieved practical capacity identified in the record. |

### Strongest rivals

**12 — Branching output; Opening × Focal × Efficacy.** Preserves focal availability for continuation. It would govern through an enlargement of possibilities as such. The frozen record instead specifies a restored enabling function and a criterion of practical recovery. Options return as what makes the correction adequate; they are not an independently introduced new repertoire. Opening can include restoration, so the choice is not made from the word “again” alone.

**28 — Focal adjustment; Repair × Focal × Standing.** Would leave the corrected object or formulation as result. The decisive counterfactual shows why repaired mechanical movement is insufficient to this record: recovered practical possibility must remain.

**33 — In-course correction; Repair × Distributed × Efficacy.** Would restore operation across a course. No such span is the recorded locus; future plurality does not make the recovery Distributed.

The name “Load transfer” is less transparent than the conjunction. No physical burden transfer is added to make the name literal; the canonical definition of focal corrective efficacy governs the selection.

### Diligence

**Status:** candidate 30. **Confidence:** moderate. **Uniqueness:** selected under the frozen record; independent reproducibility not established.
- **A-019-D1:** Test restored functional adequacy against enlarged availability in 12. Do not decide solely from repair vocabulary; retain the full practical recovery criterion.
- **A-019-D2:** Preserve explicitly restored possibilities without asserting actual exercise or a particular journey. Check the canonical name without importing literal load redistribution.

No earlier record or revision. Next target: A-020.

# A-020 — first-pass resolution and diligence

**Record ID:** FRESHER-A-020-2026-10-21-01. **Date / assessor:** 21 October 2026, Europe/Dublin; Fresher / OpenAI Codex.
**Source:** frozen Corpus A §15, Edition 1.1, blob `1b511a439a5007a3c4a61c36ff5417f415005e2c`. **Protocol:** achieved-identity confirmation, blob `69eb1e465b48bbc2e0fef0d315456a6ebabd2107`; workflow blob `f522e66fd3b068e0b07cee16c8e21ee99121ebeb`. A-019 was fully saved, indexed and published before this investigation.

## Gate One — source analysis

> The world's languages divide experience differently while helping people recognize familiar situations.

### Unfolding and candidates

“The world's languages” supplies a plurality. “Divide experience differently” makes their articulations divergent. “While helping people recognize familiar situations” places recognitive usefulness alongside that divergence.

The completed point holds variation in division together with recognition of the familiar. It neither makes language an impermeable prison nor asserts that all speakers divide experience alike. “Familiar” does not establish identical interpretations, identical referents for every speaker, or universal mutual translation.

1. Languages classify experience differently. Omits recognition.
2. All languages express exactly the same meanings. Contradicts or flattens the stated differences.
3. Languages' differing divisions of experience coexist with their helping people recognise familiar situations. Preserves both sides without erasing either.
4. Different categories cause people to recognise identical situations in identical ways. Adds causation and universal identity.
5. Language has newly given people a capacity they previously lacked. Adds a before/after acquisition.

Candidate 3 is strongest. The helping function is retained within the contrast; it is not denied because the whole concerns their coexistence.

### Diagnostic tests

| Test | Finding |
|---|---|
| Deletion | Remove recognition: divergence remains without its counterweight. Remove differing division: usefulness remains without the tension. |
| Alteration | “Divide identically” removes the variation. “Prevent recognition” changes coexistence into obstruction. |
| Narrowing | A single language alone cannot establish the claimed cross-language variation. Recognition of wholly unfamiliar situations changes the particular recognitive contribution. |
| Reversal | Recognition hindering linguistic division does not preserve the relation; the original does not assert either direction as a causal law. |
| Dependency | Each side acquires its pointed significance alongside the other: division differs without eliminating recognitive usefulness. Neither physically produces the other as asserted cause. |
| Sufficiency-location | The contrast requires the language plurality and both aspects of their activity; no single language or privileged category suffices. |
| Sufficiency versus function | Helping recognition is explicitly supplied, but no new recognitive capacity, comparative improvement or actual recognition event is the governing result. The completed point is its coexistence with divergent divisions. |
| Minimal invalidation | Make differing divisions prevent familiar recognition: the divergence remains but the particular coexistence is defeated. |

### Six questions

| Question | Answer |
|---|---|
| What was unresolved? | How differing linguistic divisions stand with languages' role in familiar recognition. |
| What changed? | Divergent articulation is established alongside recognitive usefulness. |
| What made it sufficient? | Neither variation nor familiarity cancels the other; both are retained in one account. |
| Where is sufficiency located? | Across languages and the contrast between their differing divisions and recognitive role. |
| What remains achieved? | The contextual distinction between divergent articulation and retained recognitive usefulness. |
| What would invalidate it? | Eliminate divergence, or make it defeat familiar recognition rather than coexist with helping it. |

### Four holding tests

1. **Identity:** linguistic difference understood alongside recognition of the familiar; neither bare diversity nor bare usefulness.
2. **Constitutive dependencies:** both sides give the contrast its identity. Without the recognitive side, difference loses this specific counterpoint; without difference, recognition loses this particular setting.
3. **Operative continuation:** a helping function is asserted and preserved, but the governing completion is not acquisition of a new ability or an ensuing sequence of recognition. No universal recognition or translation success is added.
4. **Minimal invalidation:** replace helping with preventing recognition; both language and diversity can remain while this contrasted coexistence fails.

### Achieved-identity discrimination

**What has been established is:** different linguistic divisions coexist with languages' helping familiar recognition.

**What obtains through what is:** the significance of divergent division obtains against the retained recognitive function, and that function is understood without assuming identical divisions.

The whole is not merely two independent facts: their coexistence distinguishes variation in articulation from loss of recognisability. Their contextual contrast constitutes this particular significance. No physical sustaining dependence or causal inference is required.

### Frozen Centre Confirmation Record

**Confirmed Operative Centre:** **Across the world's languages, divergent divisions of experience coexist with helping people recognise familiar situations, distinguishing variation in articulation from loss of recognisability.**

**Opening constraint:** the bearing of differing divisions on familiar recognition is unspecified.
**Achieved completion:** divergence and recognitive usefulness are distinguished without either being erased.
**Constitutive organisation:** the variation is understood against the retained helping function, and familiar recognition is not equated with identical divisions.
**Location of sufficiency:** across the languages and the contrasted aspects; no single locus is sufficient.
**Mode of holding:** **What remains achieved is the contrasted significance of divergent linguistic articulation alongside familiar recognition; it holds as this achievement because each aspect is understood in the other's presence without being collapsed into it; removing either aspect would leave diversity or usefulness alone without this particular determination.**
**Decisive counterfactual:** different divisions prevent rather than help familiar recognition.
**Confirmation:** preserves linguistic plurality, differing divisions, helping and familiarity; adds neither universal translation, identical meanings, actual recognition events nor causal necessity; identifies one contextual contrast.

**OPERATIVE CENTRE CONFIRMED — SOURCE SENTENCE CLOSED.**

## Gate Two — frozen record and canonical reference only

**Canonical authority:** THE_54_CONTROL.md, blob `113cdf0c50a2eb8645189f0505fa8a9584028322`.

### Unified conjunction

**05 — Context-bound mark; Differentiation × Distributed × Bearing.**

The distinction between divergent articulation and recognitive usefulness becomes determinate across the field, with its significance constituted through their coexistence.

### Alternatives

| Dimension | Assessment |
|---|---|
| Transformation | Differentiation fits keeping variation in articulation distinct from loss of recognisability. Relating is the strongest cross-pattern challenge, but connection alone omits the distinction the frozen account makes operative. Opening no enlarged availability as governing result; Repair no corrected failure; Integration no organisation into a common whole; Settlement no discharged demand. |
| Topology | Distributed fits plurality across languages and contrasted aspects without a privileged sufficient locus. Focal would select a source language or category absent from the record. Release would invent prior governance being removed. |
| Persistence | Bearing fits the aspects' contextual significance through coexistence. Standing would retain two established facts while dropping their recorded mutual qualification. Efficacy would make achieved recognition or a changed recognitive capacity govern, contrary to the frozen result's contextual contrast. |

### Strongest rivals

**23 — Reciprocal shift; Relating × Distributed × Bearing.** Preserves contextual interdependence but makes relation itself govern. The frozen account identifies a distinction: variation in division is not collapsed into loss of recognition, and recognition is not collapsed into identical division. That specific discrimination favours 05.

**06 — Field separation; Differentiation × Distributed × Efficacy.** The helping function is not ignored. However, the record does not make newly executable recognition, improved capacity or actions governed by distinctions the completed identity. It makes that function one constitutive side of the contrasted significance. Practical function in the subject matter is not automatically the whole's persistence.

**04 — Scope standing; Differentiation × Distributed × Standing.** Would retain an established differentiation. The frozen holding statement instead retains the aspects' significance through their juxtaposition, rather than independent statements about diversity and usefulness.

“Context-bound mark” names a distinction constituted in its context. No literal mark, common world-language or universal translation is inferred.

### Diligence

**Status:** candidate 05. **Confidence:** moderate. **Uniqueness:** selected under this frozen record; independent reproducibility not established.
- **A-020-D1:** Independently test 05 against 23: does the contextual distinction govern rather than their relating as such? Do not settle it by the connective alone.
- **A-020-D2:** Test 05 against 06 while preserving the explicitly stated helping function. Neither infer a newly acquired capacity nor discard the function because the record governs through contrast.

No earlier record or revision. This completes the owner's authorised batch through A-020. Next unworked sentence: A-021.

# A-021 — first-pass resolution and diligence

**Record ID:** FRESHER-A-021-2026-10-21-01. **Date / assessor:** 21 October 2026, Europe/Dublin; Fresher / OpenAI Codex.
**Source:** frozen Corpus A §15, Edition 1.1 workbook, blob `1b511a439a5007a3c4a61c36ff5417f415005e2c`. Exact wording verified against the live source. **Protocol:** achieved-identity confirmation, blob `69eb1e465b48bbc2e0fef0d315456a6ebabd2107`; workflow blob `f522e66fd3b068e0b07cee16c8e21ee99121ebeb`. A-001–A-020 are indexed; this is the next unworked sentence. The owner authorised continuation, announced here as a sequential batch through A-027.

## Gate One — source analysis

> A hospital at midnight holds emergencies, recoveries, farewells, and beginnings under one roof.

### Unfolding and competing Centres

“A hospital at midnight” gives the place and time. “Holds” introduces what belongs within that setting. Emergencies, recoveries, farewells and beginnings articulate different situations, some moving in contrary directions. “Under one roof” completes their common gathering.

1. A hospital contains events. Too general: loses the range and its particular gathering.
2. A hospital resolves every emergency. Adds successful treatment and erases the other situations.
3. At midnight, emergencies, recoveries, farewells and beginnings are gathered within one hospital. Preserves the setting, range and common place.
4. All the situations form one coordinated medical procedure. Invents functional coordination and shared purpose.

Candidate 3 survives. “Beginnings” and “farewells” are not narrowed to births and deaths; those are plausible examples, not necessary literal equivalents. Midnight does not establish that these events occur only at night.

### Verification tests

| Test | Finding |
|---|---|
| Deletion | Remove the named situations: a container remains without its articulated character. Remove common location: varied events remain without their hospital gathering. |
| Alteration | Replace the range with recoveries alone: the setting loses its juxtaposed diversity. |
| Narrowing | Replace one roof with separate unrelated hospitals: the common gathering is lost. |
| Reversal | Four separate sites each holding a hospital changes the organisation, rather than preserving it by reordered wording. |
| Dependency | The activities belong together here through their common setting, not through a stated causal cycle or mutual support. |
| Sufficiency-location | The hospital as one shared place constitutes the gathering. No one listed situation alone establishes the complete scene. |
| Minimal invalidation | Keep all four types and the time, but disperse them so no one hospital gathers them: this particular whole disappears. |

### Six confirmation questions

| Question | Determination |
|---|---|
| What was unresolved? | What the midnight hospital holds and how those situations belong together. |
| What changed? | A range of distinct situations is made intelligible as one hospital gathering. |
| What made it sufficient? | Their differentiated presence receives common membership under one roof. |
| Where is sufficiency located? | At the hospital as the common gathering place. |
| What remains achieved? | An articulated midnight hospital scene containing the named range together. |
| What would invalidate it? | Remove their common hospital setting while retaining the separate situations. |

### Four holding tests

1. **Identity:** one gathered scene with four named kinds of situation, not a mere list or one outcome.
2. **Constitutive dependencies:** the range and shared place identify this scene. The source does not establish that the situations sustain one another or depend on a separately described anchoring mechanism.
3. **Operative continuation:** no achieved capacity of the hospital, successful treatment or subsequent action is asserted. Medical usefulness cannot supply those claims.
4. **Minimal invalidation:** retain the range but remove common membership in one hospital; the particular gathering is defeated.

### Achieved-identity discrimination

**What has been established is:** one midnight hospital gathering of emergencies, recoveries, farewells and beginnings.

**What obtains through what is:** those situations' common membership obtains through sharing the hospital setting.

The second identifies the organisation of the scene, not a further support on which an otherwise achieved coherence is maintained. The result is the gathered configuration itself. Common setting is constitutive of completion; this does not alone establish a separate sustaining dependency.

### Frozen Centre Confirmation Record

**Confirmed Operative Centre:** **At midnight, emergencies, recoveries, farewells and beginnings are gathered within one hospital under a common roof.**

**Opening constraint:** the hospital scene's particular contents and common organisation remain unspecified.
**Achieved completion:** the named diverse situations form one gathered hospital scene.
**Constitutive organisation:** distinct situations have common membership at one place while retaining their differences.
**Location of sufficiency:** the hospital as the privileged common gathering place.
**Mode of holding:** **What remains achieved is one midnight hospital scene gathering emergencies, recoveries, farewells and beginnings; it holds as this achievement because the named diversity is organised within the common hospital setting; removing that shared setting would leave separate situations without this gathered whole.**
**Decisive counterfactual:** all named situations occur at midnight but no one hospital gathers them.
**Confirmation:** preserves time, place, range and unity; adds no functional coordination, exclusive night-time occurrence or specific birth/death event; identifies one complete scene.

**OPERATIVE CENTRE CONFIRMED — SOURCE SENTENCE CLOSED.**

## Gate Two — frozen record and canonical reference only

**Canonical authority:** THE_54_CONTROL.md, blob `113cdf0c50a2eb8645189f0505fa8a9584028322`.

### Unified conjunction

**37 — Focal whole; Integration × Focal × Standing.**

Distinct situations receive determinate common membership through a gathering place, and that articulated scene remains as the result. The shared place constitutes why this diversity counts as one scene.

### Structural alternatives

| Dimension | Assessment from the frozen record |
|---|---|
| Transformation | Integration fits diversity organised as one scene. Relating is the strongest cross-pattern rival: separate location-attributions omit the record's gathered whole. Differentiation would foreground differences alone; Opening establishes no access; Repair supplies no criterion failure; Settlement supplies no discharged demand. |
| Topology | Focal: the common hospital is constitutive of one gathering. Distributed would foreground articulation across parts without a privileged gathering locus; the record explicitly identifies that locus. Release has no displaced governance. |
| Persistence | Standing: the scene itself is the achieved configuration. Bearing would add support-constituted coherence beyond common placement. Efficacy would add coordinated care or achieved clinical capacity. |

### Strongest rivals

**40 — Role array; Integration × Distributed × Standing.** The range is necessary, but range alone does not select Distributed. The frozen location of sufficiency is common membership at one place, not complementary roles articulated across a system.

**38 — Frame-held whole; Integration × Focal × Bearing.** A place can be essential to identify a gathering without a distinct sustaining support constituting its coherence. The holding statement retains the gathering as its result.

**19 — Direct bearing; Relating × Focal × Standing.** Would establish which events bear a location relation to a hospital. The frozen achievement is the hospital scene as a gathered whole; reducing it to independent placements drops that organisation.

“Focal whole” captures the common scene, not medical coordination or successful treatment.

### Diligence and status

**Status:** candidate 37. **Confidence:** moderate. **Uniqueness:** selected under this frozen account; independent reproducibility is not established.
- **A-021-D1:** Test whether the recorded common membership establishes an integrated scene rather than an inventory of co-located events; do not invent functional roles to secure Integration.
- **A-021-D2:** Compare Focal with Distributed while preserving the explicit location-of-sufficiency record. Numerous events do not alone determine topology.

# A-022 — first-pass resolution and diligence

**Record ID:** FRESHER-A-022-2026-10-21-01. **Date / assessor:** 21 October 2026, Europe/Dublin; Fresher / OpenAI Codex.
**Source:** frozen Corpus A §15, Edition 1.1, blob `1b511a439a5007a3c4a61c36ff5417f415005e2c`. **Protocol:** achieved-identity confirmation, blob `69eb1e465b48bbc2e0fef0d315456a6ebabd2107`; workflow blob `f522e66fd3b068e0b07cee16c8e21ee99121ebeb`. A-021 was fully saved and publicly indexed before this investigation.

## Gate One — source analysis

> Clouds do not consult calendars before bringing rain to waiting fields.

### Unfolding and candidate Centres

Clouds are presented as not consulting calendars. The continuation specifies the relevant activity: bringing rain to fields that wait for it. The personification separates a natural arrival from a human timetable while retaining the demand for rain.

1. Clouds cannot read. Too literal and incomplete; loses rainfall and waiting fields.
2. Rain never arrives on a desired date. Too strong; independence from consultation does not exclude coincidence or predictability.
3. Rain comes to waiting fields without being answerable to human calendar consultation. Preserves the natural arrival, human temporal frame and unmet authority of that frame.
4. Rain has escaped a calendar that once controlled it. Invents former control and a change of regime.

Candidate 3 survives. “Waiting” need not attribute consciousness to fields; it locates rainfall against an agricultural expectation. No drought, harvest failure or eventual satisfaction of every waiting field is asserted.

### Verification tests

| Test | Finding |
|---|---|
| Deletion | Remove calendars: rain-to-fields remains without the human scheduling contrast. Remove waiting fields: lack of consultation remains without its practical setting of anticipated rain. |
| Alteration | Make clouds follow calendar instructions: the independence disappears. Make calendars forecast rain: prediction is different from rain depending on consultation. |
| Narrowing | Say rain happened not to follow one date on one occasion: a general non-subordination becomes a particular mismatch. |
| Reversal | Humans consulting clouds to schedule work does not establish the original direction of non-subordination. |
| Dependency | Human dates and rainfall identify what is distinguished; the rain is not constituted by consultation or by its absence as an enabling support. |
| Sufficiency-location | Completion requires natural arrival and the human scheduling frame to be distinguished in the waiting-fields setting. Neither alone establishes this contrast of authority. |
| Historical test | Nothing establishes former calendar control being removed. Non-consultation is present from the description's outset. |
| Minimal invalidation | Make calendar consultation a prerequisite for bringing the rain: the established independence fails. |

### Six confirmation questions

| Question | Answer |
|---|---|
| What was unresolved? | Whether rain's arrival to waiting fields is answerable to human calendar scheduling. |
| What changed? | Natural arrival is distinguished from consultation of human timetables. |
| What made it sufficient? | The calendar's non-authority is specified for rain arriving where it is awaited. |
| Where is sufficiency located? | Across the natural arrival and human temporal frame within that practical setting. |
| What remains achieved? | A determined distinction: calendar consultation does not govern the arrival of this rain. |
| What would invalidate it? | Calendar consultation becoming a necessary governing step for rainfall's arrival. |

### Four holding tests

1. **Identity:** the determined independence of rain-to-fields from human calendar consultation.
2. **Constitutive dependencies:** both domains identify this independence, but neither a sustaining comparison nor a conditional support makes the rainfall obtain. The achievement is the distinction itself.
3. **Operative continuation:** no new power to proceed, successful crop, liberated rain-making process or altered scheduling practice is achieved. No reader's practical lesson is imported.
4. **Minimal invalidation:** require calendar consultation for rainfall's arrival; that changes the independence while retaining rain and dates.

### Achieved-identity discrimination

**What has been established is:** bringing rain to waiting fields does not answer to calendar consultation.

**What obtains through what is:** the distinction is specified by setting natural arrival against the human scheduling frame.

The second states how the independence is identified. It does not establish that the result remains dependent on an ongoing comparison or a support supplied by calendars. The natural and calendrical domains stand distinguished; no former calendrical authority has been overcome.

### Frozen Centre Confirmation Record

**Confirmed Operative Centre:** **Rain brought by clouds to waiting fields is distinguished from human calendar scheduling: calendar consultation does not govern its arrival.**

**Opening constraint:** the authority of the human scheduling frame over awaited rain is unspecified.
**Achieved completion:** natural arrival and calendar consultation stand distinguished in their authority.
**Constitutive organisation:** the human temporal frame is shown not to direct the natural arrival awaited by the fields.
**Location of sufficiency:** across that distinction between arrival and scheduling in the waiting-fields setting.
**Mode of holding:** **What remains achieved is the determined independence of rain's arrival to waiting fields from calendar consultation; it holds as this achievement because natural arrival and human scheduling stand distinguished rather than the former being governed by the latter; removing that independence by making consultation a prerequisite would defeat this determination.**
**Decisive counterfactual:** bringing the rain requires calendar consultation.
**Confirmation:** preserves clouds, calendars, rain, waiting fields and non-consultation; adds neither universal unpredictability nor former calendar control; identifies one distinction of authority.

**OPERATIVE CENTRE CONFIRMED — SOURCE SENTENCE CLOSED.**

## Gate Two — frozen record and canonical reference only

**Canonical authority:** THE_54_CONTROL.md, blob `113cdf0c50a2eb8645189f0505fa8a9584028322`.

### Unified conjunction

**04 — Scope standing; Differentiation × Distributed × Standing.**

Natural arrival and human scheduling become determinately distinguishable across their respective domains, and their determined independence remains as the result.

### Structural alternatives

| Dimension | Assessment from the frozen record |
|---|---|
| Transformation | Differentiation fits the distinction of authority. Relating would make a connection or ordering govern; the recorded achievement is non-conflation of natural arrival with scheduling authority. Opening no enlarged availability; Repair no correction relative to a supplied criterion; Integration no organised whole; Settlement no discharged live demand. |
| Topology | Distributed fits the explicit across-domain distinction. Focal would gather completion around a privileged attribute locus instead of the recorded comparison of domains. Release would require former governing force losing authority; the record expressly establishes no prior calendar control. |
| Persistence | Standing fits the determined independence itself. Bearing would make contextual comparative significance or sustaining support constitutive of the result; the record instead distinguishes domains as an established determination. Efficacy would invent a newly accomplished freedom, function or successful agricultural continuation. |

### Strongest rivals

**05 — Context-bound mark; Differentiation × Distributed × Bearing.** The calendar frame is indispensable to stating this independence, but the frozen holding statement identifies a distinction left established. It does not identify rain's significance as remaining constituted through comparison with that frame. Necessity of the terms alone does not select Bearing.

**07 — Opposed standing; Differentiation × Release × Standing.** The result is independence, but the record denies any prior calendar governance being removed. Negative wording and non-subordination are insufficient to establish Release.

**01 — Focal mark; Differentiation × Focal × Standing.** Could attribute calendar-indifference to a privileged source. The frozen record instead locates sufficiency across natural arrival and human scheduling, which an isolated source description would not complete.

“Scope standing” captures the established distinction across the two domains. Its name is not evidence for literal geographical scope.

### Diligence

**Status:** candidate 04. **Confidence:** moderate. **Uniqueness:** selected under this frozen account; independent reproducibility is not established.
- **A-022-D1:** Independently test whether the achieved distinction stands as independence, or its significance remains context-constituted as in 05. Do not decide from term-deletion alone.
- **A-022-D2:** Test the recorded across-domain locus against focal attribution without selecting topology from grammar. Preserve the absence of any established prior governance.

No earlier record or revision. Next target: A-023.

# A-023 — first-pass resolution and diligence

**Record ID:** FRESHER-A-023-2026-10-21-01. **Date / assessor:** 21 October 2026, Europe/Dublin; Fresher / OpenAI Codex.
**Source:** frozen Corpus A §15, Edition 1.1, blob `1b511a439a5007a3c4a61c36ff5417f415005e2c`. **Protocol:** achieved-identity confirmation, blob `69eb1e465b48bbc2e0fef0d315456a6ebabd2107`; workflow blob `f522e66fd3b068e0b07cee16c8e21ee99121ebeb`. A-022 was fully saved, indexed and published before this investigation.

## Gate One — source analysis

> The smallest village may contain disagreements as intricate as those of capitals.

### Unfolding and candidate Centres

“The smallest village” brings an extreme of settlement scale into view. “May contain disagreements” asserts possibility rather than a universal fact or a particular occurrence. “As intricate as those of capitals” gives that possible complexity a comparative determination.

1. Villages have disagreements. Omits modality and comparative intricacy.
2. All villages have disagreements identical to capital disputes. Adds universality, actuality and identity of content.
3. Disagreements in even the smallest village may match those of capitals in intricacy. Preserves possibility, extreme scale and the specified dimension of comparison.
4. Small settlements cause more disagreement than cities. Invents causation and reverses the comparative determination.

Candidate 3 survives. Matching intricacy does not mean sharing subjects, number of parties or political importance. “May” does not newly grant a village an operative permission; it states possible complexity.

### Verification tests

| Test | Finding |
|---|---|
| Deletion | Remove the capital comparison: general potential intricacy remains without its specified level. Remove the village: capital complexity alone loses the cross-scale determination. |
| Alteration | “Less intricate” changes the possible level. “As frequent” changes the comparison dimension from intricacy to frequency. |
| Narrowing | Replace the smallest village with a large town: loses the extreme small-scale scope. Replace “may” with “does”: overstates actuality. |
| Reversal | Mere reversal of the equality wording can preserve parity, but moving the possibility claim to an unrelated class changes what is being asserted as possible. The original is not a causal sequence. |
| Dependency | The village disagreements' possible degree is specified through the capital counterparts, rather than supplied as an independent absolute complexity level. |
| Sufficiency-location | Comparison spans the village and capital cases; neither case alone establishes matched intricacy across scale. |
| Minimal invalidation | Rule out village disagreements ever reaching capital-level intricacy: the stated possibility is defeated. |

### Six confirmation questions

| Question | Answer |
|---|---|
| What was unresolved? | How intricate disagreement in the smallest settlement can be relative to capital disagreements. |
| What changed? | Capital-level intricacy is established as possible even there. |
| What made it sufficient? | The village disagreements' possible level is determined through parity on the stated dimension. |
| Where is sufficiency located? | Across the two settlement cases and the comparative dimension. |
| What remains achieved? | A possible level of village disagreement constituted as matching capital disagreements in intricacy. |
| What would invalidate it? | Deny that such intricacy is possible in the smallest village. |

### Four holding tests

1. **Identity:** possible parity of intricacy across the specified extreme of settlement scale; not an actual matched pair of disputes.
2. **Constitutive dependencies:** capital disagreements supply the particular level with which the village case may match. Remove that counterpart and only unspecified intricacy remains.
3. **Operative continuation:** no new capacity to disagree, ability to solve a dispute or changed political conduct is achieved. The possibility is descriptive, not a granted opportunity.
4. **Minimal invalidation:** make the smallest-village case incapable of the specified comparative intricacy; the possible parity fails.

### Achieved-identity discrimination

**What has been established is:** even the smallest village may contain capital-level intricacy of disagreement.

**What obtains through what is:** the village case's possible level of intricacy is determined through matching the capital counterparts.

The achievement concerns a possible degree specified by comparison, rather than an independently measured degree later arranged alongside another. A detached “very intricate” statement omits the positive determination of the possible level. The counterpart is constitutive of that level's identity, not evidence for a separately given absolute measurement. Necessity of mentioning two terms alone would not decide this; the comparative degree itself is the determination.

### Frozen Centre Confirmation Record

**Confirmed Operative Centre:** **Disagreements in the smallest village may attain an intricacy determined as matching that of disagreements in capitals.**

**Opening constraint:** the possible intricacy of small-settlement disagreement relative to the capital case is unspecified.
**Achieved completion:** parity with capital-level intricacy is established as possible.
**Constitutive organisation:** the possible level is determined through comparison on intricacy, across the settlement cases.
**Location of sufficiency:** across the two cases; neither alone specifies the parity.
**Mode of holding:** **What remains achieved is the smallest village disagreements' possible capital-level intricacy; it holds as this achievement because that possible level is constituted as matching the capital counterparts on the specified dimension; removing the comparative counterpart would leave only an unspecified possibility of intricacy rather than this determined possible level.**
**Decisive counterfactual:** capital-level intricacy is impossible for disagreements in the smallest village.
**Confirmation:** preserves may, smallest, disagreements, intricacy and capitals; adds no actual paired disputes, same content, causation or superiority; identifies one possible comparative determination.

**OPERATIVE CENTRE CONFIRMED — SOURCE SENTENCE CLOSED.**

## Gate Two — frozen record and canonical reference only

**Canonical authority:** THE_54_CONTROL.md, blob `113cdf0c50a2eb8645189f0505fa8a9584028322`.

### Unified conjunction

**23 — Reciprocal shift; Relating × Distributed × Bearing.**

A possible degree in one case is constituted through its correspondence with the counterpart case across the comparative field. The possibility and its specified level are one achieved determination.

### Structural alternatives

| Dimension | Assessment from the frozen record |
|---|---|
| Transformation | Relating fits a comparative degree determined through parity. Differentiation is the strongest cross-pattern challenge, but highlighting a difference of settlement scale does not establish the specified possible degree. Opening would add enlarged availability; Repair adds a failed criterion; Integration adds a whole; Settlement adds a discharged demand. |
| Topology | Distributed: the record expressly makes parity sufficient across both cases. Focal would require one privileged case or anchor sufficient for completion; neither alone determines the correspondence. Release has no prior governance being displaced. |
| Persistence | Bearing: the possible level is identified as matching its counterpart. Standing would retain a possible comparative configuration without the record's degree-through-counterpart identity. Efficacy would mistake descriptive possibility for a newly achieved operative capacity. |

### Strongest rivals

**22 — Sequential link; Relating × Distributed × Standing.** Could retain the possibility of a matching configuration. The frozen account instead identifies what degree the village case may have through the capital counterpart, rather than independently given degrees simply ordered or grouped. The positive constitution of a possible comparative level is the reason for 23; deletion of a comparison term alone would not suffice.

**05 — Context-bound mark; Differentiation × Distributed × Bearing.** Would foreground a contextual distinction between settlement size and dispute intricacy. The recorded achievement does more specifically determine possible intricacy through correspondence. The scale contrast supplies context rather than replacing that parity.

**20 — Pivoted balance; Relating × Focal × Bearing.** Would privilege the village as the sufficient relational locus. The frozen record identifies the cross-case correspondence as constitutive of completion.

“Reciprocal shift” does not imply that either dispute physically changes the other. The conjunction concerns their comparative determination; no actual matched pair is asserted.

### Diligence and status

**Status:** candidate 23. **Confidence:** moderate. **Uniqueness:** selected under this frozen record; independent reproducibility is not established.
- **A-023-D1:** Test whether degree-through-counterpart positively distinguishes 23 from an established possible comparison in 22. Do not decide merely from comparative wording.
- **A-023-D2:** Preserve descriptive modality and cross-case sufficiency without inventing actual equal disputes, biological capacities, new permissions or changed conduct.

# A-024 — first-pass resolution and diligence

**Record ID:** FRESHER-A-024-2026-10-21-01. **Date / assessor:** 21 October 2026, Europe/Dublin; Fresher / OpenAI Codex.
**Source:** frozen Corpus A §15, Edition 1.1, blob `1b511a439a5007a3c4a61c36ff5417f415005e2c`. **Protocol:** achieved-identity confirmation, blob `69eb1e465b48bbc2e0fef0d315456a6ebabd2107`; workflow blob `f522e66fd3b068e0b07cee16c8e21ee99121ebeb`. A-023 was fully saved, indexed and published before this investigation.

## Gate One — source analysis

> A lighthouse matters most when darkness makes the coastline difficult to distinguish.

### Unfolding and candidate Centres

A lighthouse is said to matter “most,” leaving the condition of greatest significance to be specified. “When darkness” introduces that condition, and the difficulty of distinguishing the coastline gives its particular content.

1. A lighthouse exists in darkness. Loses comparative importance and the stated difficulty.
2. A lighthouse always makes the coastline completely visible. Adds successful illumination and universality.
3. A lighthouse has greatest significance under darkness-induced difficulty in distinguishing the coastline. Preserves importance, its maximum and the specifying condition.
4. A ship avoids collision because of the lighthouse. Adds ship, action and successful outcome.

Candidate 3 survives. “Most” is not “only”; importance under other conditions is not denied. “Difficult” is not “impossible.”

### Verification tests

| Test | Finding |
|---|---|
| Deletion | Remove the when-clause: greatest importance remains unspecified. Remove “most”: conditional significance remains but its heightened status changes. |
| Alteration | Replace coastline difficulty with mere clock time: loses the condition through which heightened significance is intelligible. |
| Narrowing | Restrict difficulty to one particular observer: adds a case absent from the general claim. |
| Reversal | Make the lighthouse matter least under that difficulty: reverses its relative significance. |
| Dependency | Heightened importance is determined through the condition of difficult coastline discrimination; the difficulty is not merely evidence that the lighthouse exists. |
| Sufficiency-location | The lighthouse is the particular term whose significance is completed by its specifying condition. No coordinated network or range of separate lights is asserted. |
| Operative restraint | The sentence does not assert an actual sighting, successful navigation, restored visibility or collision prevention. |
| Minimal invalidation | Keep darkness-induced coastline difficulty but make the lighthouse no more important there than elsewhere: the particular maximum fails. |

### Six confirmation questions

| Question | Answer |
|---|---|
| What was unresolved? | Under what condition the lighthouse has greatest importance. |
| What changed? | Its greatest significance is located in darkness-induced difficulty distinguishing the coastline. |
| What made it sufficient? | The importance is specified through the need-condition rather than left as an unqualified value. |
| Where is sufficiency located? | At the lighthouse's significance as the particular term completed through that condition. |
| What remains achieved? | Its greatest importance under that specified difficulty. |
| What would invalidate it? | The condition holds but no longer gives the lighthouse its greatest importance. |

### Four holding tests

1. **Identity:** condition-relative greatest significance, not bare existence or performed guidance.
2. **Constitutive dependencies:** the difficulty specifies what this heightened importance consists in. Remove that condition and this particular importance attribution loses its identity.
3. **Operative continuation:** practical significance is asserted, but no completed navigation, visual success or newly achieved capacity is identified. Plausible usefulness is not substituted for such an achievement.
4. **Minimal invalidation:** retain difficulty while remove the lighthouse's heightened importance under it.

### Achieved-identity discrimination

**What has been established is:** the lighthouse matters most under darkness-induced difficulty in distinguishing the coastline.

**What obtains through what is:** its heightened significance obtains through that specified need-condition.

The latter explains the importance's condition-relative identity rather than an external evidential reason for an independent value. The lighthouse's existence could remain unchanged while this greatest-significance relation failed. No physical action by the lighthouse is needed to make this the particular conditional attribution.

### Frozen Centre Confirmation Record

**Confirmed Operative Centre:** **A lighthouse's greatest significance obtains under the condition that darkness makes the coastline difficult to distinguish.**

**Opening constraint:** the condition of greatest significance is unspecified.
**Achieved completion:** the lighthouse's heightened importance is determined through the coastline-discrimination difficulty.
**Constitutive organisation:** a particular term's significance is constituted relative to the specified need-condition.
**Location of sufficiency:** the lighthouse's significance as the privileged term whose determination is completed through that condition.
**Mode of holding:** **What remains achieved is the lighthouse's greatest significance under darkness-induced difficulty distinguishing the coastline; it holds as this achievement because the specified need-condition constitutes that heightened importance; removing its significance under that condition would leave the lighthouse without this particular importance attribution.**
**Decisive counterfactual:** the difficulty persists but no longer gives the lighthouse heightened importance.
**Confirmation:** preserves greatest, darkness, coastline and difficulty; adds neither only-at-night importance nor actual guidance, impossibility or prevented collision; identifies one condition-relative attribution.

**OPERATIVE CENTRE CONFIRMED — SOURCE SENTENCE CLOSED.**

## Gate Two — frozen record and canonical reference only

**Canonical authority:** THE_54_CONTROL.md, blob `113cdf0c50a2eb8645189f0505fa8a9584028322`.

### Unified conjunction

**20 — Pivoted balance; Relating × Focal × Bearing.**

A particular term acquires its heightened significance through the condition that constitutes why it matters. That focal condition-relative importance is the one completed identity.

### Structural alternatives

| Dimension | Assessment from the frozen record |
|---|---|
| Transformation | Relating fits importance through a specified condition. Differentiation would foreground comparison alone, dropping what constitutes the focal significance. Opening has no achieved access; Repair no correction; Integration no organised contributions; Settlement no discharged demand. |
| Topology | Focal fits the privileged importance-attribution. Distributed would require a constitutive field or span beyond completing this term through its condition. Release has no former governance losing force. |
| Persistence | Bearing fits condition-constituted significance. Standing would leave an established link without the recorded importance-through-need identity. Efficacy would infer an accomplished guidance function or capacity beyond the confirmed attribution. |

### Strongest rivals

**19 — Direct bearing; Relating × Focal × Standing.** Would retain a definite connection to a condition. The frozen record identifies more specifically an importance that obtains through that condition; a bare connection is not the particular completed identity.

**21 — Responsive return; Relating × Focal × Efficacy.** Practical significance is not denied, but no achieved operative capacity or resulting success is the confirmed result. Do not convert condition-relative importance into actual guidance merely because the subject has a familiar function.

**23 — Reciprocal shift; Relating × Distributed × Bearing.** The condition and focal term are both necessary, but the record locates completion in the focal attribution, not a field of counterpart standings.

“Pivoted balance” is not evidence for equal forces. The conjunction describes significance constituted through a specifying counterpart condition.

### Diligence and status

**Status:** candidate 20. **Confidence:** medium-high in conditional significance; moderate in topology. **Uniqueness:** selected under this frozen record; independent reproducibility is not established.
- **A-024-D1:** Test 20 against 21 while retaining practical importance without importing performed guidance or an achieved capacity.
- **A-024-D2:** Test focal attribution against distributed comparative standing. A two-term dependency alone does not determine topology.
